# Project brief

_Generated by BurstCode background explorer at 2026-05-22T02:14:35.929Z_

This project contains **1017** documented source files across 20 top-level areas. Per-file documentation lives under `docs/`.

## (root)

- `batch_runner.py` — Parallel batch processing engine that runs the AIAgent across dataset prompts with multiprocessing, checkpointing, and content-based resume. Extracts tool usage and reasoning statistics, normalizes schemas for HuggingFace compatibility, and merges per-batch JSONL outputs into a unified trajectories file.
- `hermes_bootstrap.py` — Windows-only UTF-8 bootstrap module. Sets `PYTHONUTF8`/`PYTHONIOENCODING` env vars and reconfigures `sys.stdout`/`stderr`/`stdin` to UTF-8 to prevent `UnicodeEncodeError` crashes on Windows consoles.
- `hermes_constants.py` — Import-safe shared constants and path resolvers for Hermes, including `HERMES_HOME` resolution, profile fallback warnings, environment detection (WSL/Container/Termux), and IPv4 preference patching.
- `hermes_logging.py` — Centralized logging setup providing rotating file handlers, session context injection, component filtering, and secret redaction across CLI, gateway, and cron modes.
- `hermes_time.py` — Timezone-aware clock helper that resolves IANA timezones from `HERMES_TIMEZONE` or `config.yaml`, caching the result for fast `now()` calls.
- `mcp_serve.py` — Stdio MCP server exposing Hermes messaging conversations as tools, backed by an `EventBridge` that polls `SessionDB` and `sessions.json` for live updates.
- `mini_swe_runner.py` — Standalone SWE task runner using Hermes-Agent execution environments (local, Docker, Modal) that outputs trajectories in Hermes format compatible with batch_runner.py and trajectory_compressor.py. Supports single-task and batch JSONL modes with a built-in terminal tool definition.
- `model_tools.py` — Thin orchestration layer over the tool registry providing async bridging, tool discovery, memoized schema generation with dynamic schema overrides, argument type coercion, and a hook-rich function call dispatcher consumed by run_agent.py, cli.py, batch_runner.py, and RL environments.
- `rl_cli.py` — Dedicated CLI runner for RL training workflows with extended timeouts, RL-focused system prompt, and full toolset including RL training tools. Supports interactive mode and environment listing.
- `toolsets.py` — Defines toolset configurations and management utilities for the Hermes agent framework, establishing how tools are grouped, validated, and exposed to the agent loop.
- `utils.py` — Provides shared utility functions for the hermes-agent module, covering JSON serialization, logging configuration, OS interactions, file permission checks, and temporary file management.

## acp_adapter

- `acp_adapter/__init__.py` — Package initialization file for the ACP adapter module. Contains only a docstring identifying the package's purpose within the hermes-agent ecosystem.
- `acp_adapter/__main__.py` — Enables execution of the ACP adapter via `python -m acp_adapter` by importing and invoking the main entry point from the `entry` module.
- `acp_adapter/auth.py` — Authentication helpers that detect the currently configured Hermes runtime provider by querying the CLI's runtime provider resolver.
- `acp_adapter/entry.py` — CLI entry point that bootstraps the environment, configures stderr-only logging, discovers MCP tools, and launches the async ACP agent server.
- `acp_adapter/events.py` — Callback factories that bridge synchronous AIAgent events to asynchronous ACP session updates, handling tool progress, reasoning, steps, and messages.
- `acp_adapter/permissions.py` — Bridges ACP permission requests to Hermes' dangerous-command approval system, mapping protocol options to internal approval strings and handling timeouts.
- `acp_adapter/server.py` — Core `HermesACPAgent` implementation handling ACP lifecycle, session management, prompt execution, slash commands, model switching, and multimodal resource parsing.
- `acp_adapter/session.py` — Session management and persistence layer that maps ACP sessions to `AIAgent` instances, handles Windows/WSL path translation, and syncs state to SQLite.
- `acp_adapter/tools.py` — Maps Hermes tool names to ACP ToolKind categories, builds human-readable tool-call titles, formats structured completion results into Markdown, and constructs ACP ToolCallStart/ToolCallProgress events with diff content for file-editing tools.

## agent

- `agent/__init__.py` — Package init for the agent module. Contains only a docstring documenting that utility functions and classes were extracted from the monolithic run_agent.py to keep the AIAgent orchestrator focused.
- `agent/account_usage.py` — Fetches and renders account usage/limit snapshots from OpenAI Codex, Anthropic (OAuth), and OpenRouter providers, exposing a unified AccountUsageSnapshot dataclass and Markdown rendering.
- `agent/anthropic_adapter.py` — Adapter translating Hermes's internal OpenAI-style message format to Anthropic's Messages API. Handles authentication (API keys, OAuth, Claude Code credentials), model-specific feature negotiation (adaptive thinking, fast mode, token limits), and third-party endpoint compatibility (Kimi, DeepSeek, Bedrock, Azure).
- `agent/bedrock_adapter.py` — AWS Bedrock Converse API adapter that bridges OpenAI-format messages and tools to Bedrock's native SDK, handling client caching, credential resolution, model discovery, and response normalization.
- `agent/codex_responses_adapter.py` — Stateless format-conversion adapter for the OpenAI Codex Responses API, translating internal chat messages, tool calls, and reasoning items into Responses API input/output structures.
- `agent/context_compressor.py` — Implements lossy context window compression by summarizing middle conversation turns with an auxiliary LLM while protecting head and tail context.
- `agent/context_engine.py` — Abstract base class defining the pluggable interface for context management engines, including token tracking, compression triggers, and session lifecycle hooks.
- `agent/context_references.py` — Parses and expands `@` references in user messages (files, folders, git diffs, URLs) into formatted context blocks, enforcing security boundaries and token limits.
- `agent/copilot_acp_client.py` — OpenAI-compatible shim that forwards requests to GitHub Copilot's ACP server via a short-lived subprocess. It formats conversation history into a single prompt, parses JSON-RPC responses, extracts tool calls from XML/JSON blocks, and enforces file-system safety by validating paths against the session CWD and redacting sensitive content.
- `agent/credential_pool.py` — Persistent credential management system supporting multi-key failover, exhaustion cooldowns, and automatic token refresh. It seeds credentials from environment variables, OAuth files, and custom providers, syncs with external auth stores to handle single-use refresh tokens, and implements selection strategies like round-robin and least-used.
- `agent/credential_sources.py` — Unified removal framework for credential sources that ensures `hermes auth remove` permanently cleans up external state and suppresses re-seeding. It registers provider-specific removal steps to prevent ad-hoc cleanup logic and guarantee idempotent credential lifecycle management.
- `agent/curator_backup.py` — Implements snapshot and rollback functionality for the ~/.hermes/skills/ directory, ensuring safe recovery before curator maintenance passes.
- `agent/curator.py` — Orchestrates background skill maintenance by auto-transitioning lifecycle states and spawning a forked LLM agent to consolidate, archive, or prune agent-created skills.
- `agent/display.py` — CLI presentation module providing animated spinners, kawaii face feedback, tool call previews, and inline unified diff rendering. Features skin-aware ANSI color resolution, lazy theme loading, and configurable preview truncation for non-blocking terminal UI updates.
- `agent/error_classifier.py` — Centralized API error taxonomy and classification pipeline that maps raw exceptions to structured `ClassifiedError` objects with recovery hints. Uses priority-ordered checks to determine retryability, credential rotation, context compression, or provider fallback.
- `agent/file_safety.py` — Shared filesystem safety rules that define write-denied paths and prefixes, enforce `HERMES_WRITE_SAFE_ROOT` boundaries, and block reads of internal cache directories to prevent prompt injection. Used by both tool implementations and ACP shims.
- `agent/gemini_cloudcode_adapter.py` — OpenAI-SDK-compatible facade that translates chat completions to Google's Cloud Code Assist API. Handles message/tool translation, OAuth PKCE authentication, project context resolution, and SSE streaming with structured error parsing.
- `agent/gemini_native_adapter.py` — OpenAI-compatible transport shim translating Hermes's standard message/tool flow into Google's native Gemini REST API, handling synchronous/asynchronous streaming, tier probing, and structured error classification.
- `agent/gemini_schema.py` — Schema sanitizer that strips OpenAI/JSON Schema keys unsupported by Gemini's `FunctionDeclaration.parameters`, recursively cleaning nested properties and coercing typed enums to strings.
- `agent/google_code_assist.py` — Control-plane client for Google's Code Assist API, managing user onboarding, tier discovery, quota retrieval, and VPC-SC fallbacks via LRO polling and endpoint failover.
- `agent/google_oauth.py` — Full OAuth 2.0 PKCE implementation for Google accounts, handling credential persistence, token refresh deduplication, callback server management, and headless paste-mode fallbacks.
- `agent/i18n.py` — Lightweight i18n module for static Hermes UI strings, loading YAML catalogs, resolving language via env/config, and providing safe fallback translation with format substitution.
- `agent/image_gen_provider.py` — Abstract base class and helper utilities for pluggable image generation backends, standardizing response shapes, aspect ratio validation, and base64 image caching.
- `agent/image_gen_registry.py` — Thread-safe registry for image generation providers, managing plugin registration, availability checks, and active provider resolution with explicit config and legacy fallback logic.
- `agent/image_routing.py` — Routing helpers that decide whether user-attached images are sent as native multimodal content parts or pre-analyzed via text-based vision. Includes MIME sniffing, base64 data-URL encoding, and OpenAI-style content part construction.
- `agent/insights.py` — Session Insights Engine that queries the SQLite state database to produce usage reports covering tokens, costs, tool/skill usage, activity patterns, and notable sessions. Formats output for terminal and gateway display.
- `agent/lmstudio_reasoning.py` — Resolves reasoning effort levels for LM Studio's OpenAI-compatible chat.completions endpoint. Maps user config to valid effort values and clamps against the model's declared allowed options.
- `agent/lsp/__init__.py` — Entry point for LSP integration. Provides a process-wide singleton `LSPService` via `get_service()`, with lazy initialization, atexit cleanup, and explicit `shutdown_service()` for manual teardown.
- `agent/lsp/cli.py` — CLI subcommand handlers for `hermes lsp`. Provides status, list, install, install-all, restart, and which commands for managing language server binaries and service state.
- `agent/lsp/client.py` — Async LSP client over stdin/stdout. Manages a single language server subprocess, JSON-RPC exchange, file synchronization, and diagnostic push/pull with deduplication and retry logic.
- `agent/lsp/eventlog.py` — Structured logging for the LSP layer with once-per-X deduplication. Prevents log spam by emitting state transitions at INFO/WARNING exactly once per session, while keeping diagnostic events at INFO every time.
- `agent/lsp/install.py` — Handles auto-installation of LSP server binaries into a Hermes-owned staging directory. Supports npm, Go, and pip strategies with thread-safe per-package locking, caching, and graceful fallback to manual or in-process checking.
- `agent/lsp/manager.py` — Orchestrates async LSP clients via a background asyncio loop. Manages client lifecycle per server/workspace pair, implements delta diagnostic baselines, and provides a synchronous API for the file operations layer.
- `agent/lsp/protocol.py` — Minimal LSP JSON-RPC 2.0 framer and envelope builder. Handles Content-Length header parsing, async stream reading, and message classification for the LSP client layer.
- `agent/lsp/reporter.py` — Formats raw LSP diagnostics into compact, severity-filtered XML-like blocks for model consumption. Defaults to errors only, caps per-file and total character limits, and handles truncation.
- `agent/lsp/servers.py` — Registry of per-language LSP server definitions. Maps file extensions to spawn builders, project root resolvers, and initialization options, with fallbacks to auto-install when binaries are missing.
- `agent/lsp/workspace.py` — Handles workspace gating and project-root resolution. Ensures LSP only runs inside git worktrees and provides a bounded upward walk for language-specific project markers.
- `agent/manual_compression_feedback.py` — Generates user-facing summaries for manual context compression operations. Compares before/after message counts and token estimates, detecting noops and explaining token inflation edge cases.
- `agent/markdown_tables.py` — Realigns markdown tables for accurate terminal rendering with CJK and wide characters. Uses display-width calculations and falls back to vertical key-value layout when tables exceed terminal width.
- `agent/memory_manager.py` — Orchestrates pluggable memory providers for the agent, enforcing a one-external-provider limit. Provides context fencing helpers, a streaming scrubber for chunked output, and lifecycle hooks for prefetch, sync, session switches, compression, and delegation.
- `agent/memory_provider.py` — Abstract base class defining the contract for pluggable memory providers. Specifies core lifecycle methods, optional hooks for session events and compression, and config schema support for the setup wizard.
- `agent/model_metadata.py` — Pure utility module for model metadata resolution, context length detection, and rough token estimation. Supports live probing of OpenRouter, custom endpoints, local servers (Ollama, LM Studio, vLLM, llama.cpp), and provider-specific APIs (Codex OAuth, Nous Portal, Anthropic).
- `agent/models_dev.py` — Integrates with the models.dev registry to fetch, cache, and query provider/model metadata. Implements a multi-tier caching strategy (in-memory, disk, network) and provides helper functions to resolve context windows, capabilities, and filter agentic models.
- `agent/moonshot_schema.py` — Sanitizes OpenAI-style JSON Schema tool parameters to comply with Moonshot/Kimi's stricter API requirements. Recursively repairs missing types, handles `anyOf` parent/child type conflicts, and strips unsupported keywords like `nullable`.
- `agent/nous_rate_guard.py` — Implements a cross-session rate limit breaker for Nous Portal using a shared JSON state file. Prevents retry amplification by recording 429 reset times and distinguishing genuine account exhaustion from transient upstream capacity issues.
- `agent/onboarding.py` — Manages contextual, one-time onboarding hints triggered by specific user behaviors. Tracks seen flags in `config.yaml` and provides tailored messages for CLI and gateway interfaces.
- `agent/plugin_llm.py` — Provides a secure, host-owned LLM facade for trusted plugins. Enforces per-plugin trust policies from `config.yaml` to gate provider/model overrides, and delegates actual inference to `agent.auxiliary_client`.
- `agent/portal_tags.py` — Centralizes product attribution tags for Nous Portal API requests. Ensures consistent `product` and `client` version tags across all Hermes code paths to prevent telemetry drift.
- `agent/prompt_builder.py` — Stateless system prompt assembly module that composes identity, platform hints, skills index, environment awareness, and context files into the agent's system prompt. Includes prompt-injection scanning for context files and a two-layer skills cache (in-process LRU + disk snapshot).
- `agent/prompt_caching.py` — Pure-function module implementing Anthropic's prompt caching strategy. Applies `cache_control` markers to the system prompt and last 3 non-system messages to reduce input token costs by ~75% within a session.
- `agent/rate_limit_tracker.py` — Parses `x-ratelimit-*` HTTP response headers into structured `RateLimitState` dataclasses and formats them for terminal/chat display. Supports the Nous Portal header convention (also used by OpenRouter and OpenAI-compatible APIs).
- `agent/redact.py` — Regex-based secret redaction engine for logs and tool output. Masks API keys, tokens, credentials, private keys, DB connection strings, JWTs, Discord mentions, and phone numbers before they reach log files or verbose output.
- `agent/retry_utils.py` — Jittered exponential backoff utility that decorrelates concurrent retries across multiple sessions hitting the same rate-limited provider, preventing thundering-herd retry spikes.
- `agent/shell_hooks.py` — Bridges external shell-script hooks into the agent lifecycle by parsing configuration and executing subprocess commands at designated trigger points.
- `agent/tool_result_classification.py` — Shared helper that determines whether a file-mutation tool result (write_file or patch) proves the write actually landed on disk, by parsing the JSON payload for success indicators.
- `agent/trajectory.py` — Utilities for saving conversation trajectories to JSONL files, including tag conversion from legacy REASONING_SCRATCHPAD to tags and completion-status-based file routing.
- `agent/transports/__init__.py` — Transport layer registry that lazily discovers and instantiates provider-specific transport classes by api_mode string, with graceful fallback for missing modules.
- `agent/transports/anthropic.py` — Anthropic Messages API transport that delegates format conversion to anthropic_adapter.py, normalizes content blocks (text, thinking, tool_use), and maps stop reasons to OpenAI equivalents.
- `agent/transports/base.py` — Abstract base class defining the transport interface: message/tool conversion, kwargs building, response normalization, validation, cache stats extraction, and finish reason mapping.
- `agent/transports/bedrock.py` — AWS Bedrock Converse API transport that delegates to bedrock_adapter.py, handles both raw boto3 dicts and pre-normalized SimpleNamespace responses, and maps Bedrock-specific stop reasons.
- `agent/transports/chat_completions.py` — OpenAI Chat Completions transport handling ~16 providers, with dual-path kwargs assembly (profile-based vs legacy flags), Gemini thinking config translation, and Codex leak sanitization.
- `agent/transports/codex_app_server_session.py` — Session adapter for the Codex app-server runtime, managing one Codex thread per Hermes session, driving turn/start, handling approval requests, and projecting events into Hermes' message format.
- `agent/transports/codex_app_server.py` — JSON-RPC 2.0 client for the Codex app-server subprocess, using newline-delimited JSON over stdio with reader threads for stdout/stderr and blocking queues for notifications.
- `agent/transports/codex_event_projector.py` — Projects Codex app-server item notifications into Hermes' OpenAI-shaped message format, maintaining message-alternation invariants and accumulating reasoning content across items.
- `agent/transports/codex.py` — OpenAI Responses API (Codex) transport that delegates to codex_responses_adapter.py, handling system prompt extraction, reasoning config, and provider-specific headers for GitHub/xAI/Codex backends.
- `agent/transports/hermes_tools_mcp_server.py` — MCP server that exposes a curated subset of Hermes tools (web search, browser automation, vision, memory, skills, kanban) to the Codex subprocess via stdio transport.
- `agent/transports/types.py` — Defines canonical dataclasses (ToolCall, Usage, NormalizedResponse) that all provider adapters normalize their API responses into, with backward-compatibility properties for legacy agent loop access patterns.
- `agent/usage_pricing.py` — Cross-provider token usage normalization and cost estimation engine with hardcoded pricing snapshots for Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, Bedrock, DeepSeek, and MiniMax models.
- `agent/video_gen_provider.py` — Abstract base class defining the pluggable video generation provider interface, with helper functions for caching video files and building uniform success/error response dicts.
- `agent/video_gen_registry.py` — Thread-safe registry for video generation providers, resolving the active provider from config.yaml with single-provider fallback.
- `agent/web_search_provider.py` — Abstract base class for web search/extract/crawl backends, defining a unified plugin-facing surface that preserves the legacy response-shape contract from tools.web_providers.base.
- `agent/web_search_registry.py` — Thread-safe registry for web search providers with multi-tier active resolution: explicit config, single-provider shortcut, and legacy preference walk filtered by capability and availability.

## cron

- `cron/__init__.py` — Package entry point for the Hermes Agent cron scheduling system. Re-exports job CRUD operations and the scheduler tick function from submodules, providing a clean public API for scheduled task management.
- `cron/jobs.py` — Core cron job storage, schedule parsing, and lifecycle management. Handles job CRUD with atomic file writes, timezone-aware scheduling (interval/cron/one-shot), grace-period catch-up for missed runs, and curator-driven skill reference rewriting.
- `cron/scheduler.py` — Orchestrates scheduled cron job execution, supporting both pure-script watchdogs and LLM-driven agents. Handles prompt assembly, skill injection, security scanning, multi-platform delivery routing, and parallel/sequential job scheduling with file-based locking.

## environments

- `environments/__init__.py` — Package initializer for the Atropos RL training integration, exposing core abstractions like `HermesAgentLoop`, `ToolContext`, and `HermesAgentBaseEnv` while gracefully handling missing dependencies.
- `environments/agent_loop.py` — Implements a reusable multi-turn agent loop for RL environments, dispatching OpenAI-spec tool calls via `handle_function_call`, managing tool execution in a thread pool, and tracking reasoning, errors, and turn budgets.
- `environments/agentic_opd_env.py` — Implements an On-Policy Distillation (OPD) environment for training agentic LLMs on coding tasks. It runs standard rollouts, extracts hindsight hints from tool results using an LLM judge, and scores student tokens under enhanced distributions via VLLM logprobs to generate dense training signals.
- `environments/benchmarks/tblite/tblite_env.py` — A lightweight evaluation environment for terminal agents, inheriting from TerminalBench 2.0 but targeting the faster, difficulty-calibrated OpenThoughts-TBLite dataset. It simplifies configuration for rapid iteration on smaller models.
- `environments/benchmarks/terminalbench_2/terminalbench2_env.py` — A comprehensive evaluation environment for Terminal-Bench 2.0 that runs agentic LLMs in isolated Docker/Modal sandboxes. It handles dataset loading, per-task image resolution, concurrent execution with timeouts, and binary reward verification via test suites.
- `environments/benchmarks/yc_bench/yc_bench_env.py` — Evaluation environment for YC-Bench, a long-horizon business simulation benchmark. Agents act as CEOs managing cash flow and tasks via CLI commands, with scoring based on survival and final funds.
- `environments/hermes_base_env.py` — Abstract base environment for Hermes-Agent integrating with Atropos. Handles two-phase server operation, toolset resolution, agent loop orchestration, and reward computation plumbing.
- `environments/hermes_swe_env/hermes_swe_env.py` — SWE-bench style environment for software engineering tasks. Uses Modal sandboxes for isolated code execution and verifies solutions by running test commands in the same sandbox.
- `environments/patches.py` — Legacy module for async compatibility patches. Currently a no-op since async safety is built into the Modal environment, but kept for backward compatibility.
- `environments/terminal_test_env/terminal_test_env.py` — Simple test environment with inline file-creation tasks. Validates the full stack end-to-end by checking if agents create files with exact expected content.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/__init__.py` — Registry and factory for client-side tool call parsers. Extracts structured tool calls from raw model output text for Phase 2 (VLLM) server mode.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/deepseek_v3_1_parser.py` — Parser for DeepSeek V3.1 tool calls. Extracts function names and arguments from custom unicode token boundaries without JSON code block wrappers.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/deepseek_v3_parser.py` — Parser for DeepSeek V3 tool calls. Handles fullwidth unicode tokens and JSON code blocks, supporting multiple simultaneous tool calls.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/glm45_parser.py` — Parser for GLM 4.5 tool calls. Extracts arguments from custom `<arg_key>/<arg_value>` XML-like tags instead of standard JSON.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/glm47_parser.py` — Parser for GLM 4.7 tool calls. Extends GLM 4.5 parser with updated regex patterns to handle newlines between key/value tags and different wrapper formats.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/hermes_parser.py` — Parser for Hermes-format tool calls. Extracts JSON payloads from `<tool_call>...</tool_call>` tags, handling both closed and truncated/unclosed generations.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/kimi_k2_parser.py` — Parser for Kimi K2 tool calls. Extracts function names from `function_id` tokens and arguments from section-wrapped blocks.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/llama_parser.py` — Parser for Llama 3.x/4 JSON-format tool calls. Finds JSON objects with `name` and `arguments`/`parameters` keys, optionally preceded by `<|python_tag|>`.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/longcat_parser.py` — Parser for Longcat Flash Chat tool calls. Identical logic to Hermes parser but uses `<longcat_tool_call>` XML tags instead of `<tool_call>`.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/mistral_parser.py` — Parser for Mistral tool calls. Supports both pre-v11 JSON array format and v11+ inline format, detecting structure dynamically.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/qwen_parser.py` — Parser for Qwen 2.5 tool calls. Reuses Hermes parser logic since both models use the identical `<tool_call>{"name": ..., "arguments": ...}</tool_call>` format.
- `environments/tool_call_parsers/qwen3_coder_parser.py` — Parser for Qwen3-Coder tool calls. Extracts arguments from nested XML-style `<function>` and `<parameter>` tags with type conversion.
- `environments/tool_context.py` — Provides a per-rollout handle granting reward and verification functions direct access to all hermes-agent tools, scoped to a specific task_id.
- `environments/web_research_env.py` — Reinforcement learning environment for training multi-step web research skills using the FRAMES benchmark or built-in fallback questions.

## gateway

- `gateway/__init__.py` — Package initializer for the Hermes Gateway, exposing configuration, session management, and delivery routing primitives.
- `gateway/builtin_hooks/__init__.py` — Placeholder package initializer for built-in gateway hooks that are automatically registered at runtime.
- `gateway/channel_directory.py` — Maintains a cached, periodically refreshed map of reachable messaging channels and contacts across connected platforms.
- `gateway/config.py` — Central configuration loader for the Hermes gateway, managing platform connections, session reset policies, streaming settings, and environment variable overrides.
- `gateway/delivery.py` — Routes cron job outputs and agent responses to messaging platforms or local files based on parsed delivery targets.
- `gateway/display_config.py` — Resolves per-platform display and verbosity settings with a tiered fallback system for tool progress, streaming, and reasoning visibility.
- `gateway/hooks.py` — Lightweight event-driven hook system that discovers and executes user-defined Python handlers for gateway lifecycle events.
- `gateway/mirror.py` — Appends delivery-mirror records to target session transcripts to maintain cross-platform message context.
- `gateway/pairing.py` — Implements a secure, code-based DM pairing system for authorizing new users on messaging platforms.
- `gateway/platform_registry.py` — Central registry for discovering and instantiating messaging platform adapters, supporting built-in and plugin extensions.
- `gateway/platforms/__init__.py` — Package initializer for messaging platform adapters, using lazy imports to defer heavy dependencies.
- `gateway/platforms/_http_client_limits.py` — Provides tuned `httpx.Limits` configuration for long-lived platform adapter HTTP clients to prevent file descriptor exhaustion.
- `gateway/platforms/bluebubbles.py` — iMessage platform adapter using the BlueBubbles macOS server for REST API communication and webhook ingestion.
- `gateway/platforms/dingtalk.py` — DingTalk chatbot adapter using Stream Mode for real-time messaging, supporting AI Cards for streaming updates, group chat gating, and media resolution via OpenAPI.
- `gateway/platforms/email.py` — Email gateway adapter polling IMAP for inbound messages and replying via SMTP, featuring attachment caching, automated-sender filtering, and thread context tracking.
- `gateway/platforms/feishu_comment_rules.py` — Feishu document comment access-control module implementing 3-tier rule resolution, mtime-cached config loading, and pairing-based user approval.
- `gateway/platforms/feishu_comment.py` — Orchestrates Feishu/Lark drive document comment handling by parsing events, fetching context, running an AIAgent with specialized tools, and delivering chunked replies.
- `gateway/platforms/helpers.py` — Provides shared utility classes for gateway platform adapters, including message deduplication, text batch aggregation, markdown stripping, and thread participation tracking.
- `gateway/platforms/homeassistant.py` — Implements a Home Assistant platform adapter that subscribes to WebSocket state-change events, filters them, and forwards formatted notifications to the agent.
- `gateway/platforms/matrix.py` — Implements a Matrix gateway adapter using the mautrix SDK, supporting E2EE, proxy routing, and reaction-based command approvals. It handles message syncing, media caching, text batching for client-side splits, and Markdown-to-HTML conversion with a regex fallback.
- `gateway/platforms/mattermost.py` — Mattermost gateway adapter implementing REST API v4 and WebSocket communication. Handles authentication, message posting, file/image uploads, mention gating, channel whitelisting, and automatic WebSocket reconnection with exponential backoff.
- `gateway/platforms/msgraph_webhook.py` — Microsoft Graph webhook adapter that hosts an aiohttp server to receive change notifications. Validates subscriptions, filters by CIDR and HMAC clientState, deduplicates receipts, and renders payloads via templates or JSON.
- `gateway/platforms/qqbot/__init__.py` — Package initialization for the QQBot platform. Re-exports core symbols from submodules to maintain backward compatibility and expose adapters, crypto utilities, chunked uploaders, and inline keyboard builders.
- `gateway/platforms/qqbot/adapter.py` — Implements the QQ Bot platform adapter, managing WebSocket connections, REST API interactions, and message routing for C2C, group, and guild contexts. It handles inline keyboard interactions for approvals, voice-to-text transcription with multiple fallbacks, and chunked media uploads.
- `gateway/platforms/qqbot/chunked_upload.py` — Implements the QQ Bot v2 chunked upload flow for files exceeding inline size limits, handling preparation, concurrent part uploads to COS, and completion with retry logic.
- `gateway/platforms/qqbot/constants.py` — Centralizes QQBot adapter configuration, including API endpoints, timeouts, retry backoffs, message limits, and media type enums.
- `gateway/platforms/qqbot/crypto.py` — Provides AES-256-GCM utilities for securely generating ephemeral bind keys and decrypting bot credentials during QR onboarding.
- `gateway/platforms/qqbot/keyboards.py` — Defines inline keyboard structures, builders, and parsers for QQ Bot approval and update-prompt interactions.
- `gateway/platforms/qqbot/onboard.py` — Implements the synchronous QR-code scan-to-configure registration flow, polling for completion and decrypting credentials.
- `gateway/platforms/qqbot/utils.py` — Supplies shared utilities for QQBot API requests, including User-Agent construction, header generation, and config coercion.
- `gateway/platforms/signal_rate_limit.py` — Implements a process-wide token-bucket scheduler to manage Signal attachment rate limits and prevent 429 errors.
- `gateway/platforms/signal.py` — Signal messenger adapter connecting to a signal-cli HTTP daemon. Handles inbound messages via SSE streaming and outbound actions via JSON-RPC 2.0, featuring markdown-to-Signal formatting, typing indicators with exponential backoff, reaction hooks, attachment caching, and recipient UUID resolution.
- `gateway/platforms/sms.py` — Twilio SMS adapter using an aiohttp webhook server for inbound messages and the Twilio REST API for outbound. Implements HMAC-SHA1 signature validation, message chunking, basic auth, and echo prevention for multi-tenant phone number routing.
- `gateway/platforms/telegram_network.py` — Network resilience helpers for Telegram providing a fallback HTTP transport. Discovers reachable API IPs via DNS-over-HTTPS, preserves TLS SNI, and caches sticky fallback addresses when the primary domain is unreachable.
- `gateway/platforms/webhook.py` — Generic webhook receiver adapter running an aiohttp server. Validates HMAC signatures, renders prompt templates, enforces idempotency and rate limits, and routes agent responses cross-platform or via direct delivery.
- `gateway/platforms/wecom_callback.py` — HTTP callback adapter for WeCom self-built enterprise apps that decrypts encrypted XML payloads, deduplicates inbound messages, and routes them to the agent queue while acknowledging requests immediately.
- `gateway/platforms/wecom_crypto.py` — AES-CBC encryption and signature verification module compatible with Tencent's WeCom BizMsgCrypt SDK, used to secure callback payloads.
- `gateway/platforms/wecom.py` — WebSocket-based adapter for WeCom AI Bots that handles subscription, inbound message parsing, text batching, media upload/download, and granular access policies.
- `gateway/platforms/weixin.py` — Implements the Weixin (WeChat) platform adapter for Hermes, connecting to personal accounts via Tencent's iLink Bot API. It manages long-polling for inbound messages, AES-128-ECB encrypted media CDN transfers, QR-code login, and Markdown-to-WeChat formatting adaptations.
- `gateway/platforms/whatsapp.py` — WhatsApp adapter bridging Python to a Node.js Web client via HTTP. Manages subprocess lifecycle, polls messages, caches media, formats markdown, and enforces DM/group access policies.
- `gateway/platforms/yuanbao_media.py` — Yuanbao platform media module handling COS uploads, URL downloads, pure-Python image dimension parsing, and Tencent IM message body construction.
- `gateway/platforms/yuanbao_proto.py` — Hand-rolled protobuf codec for Yuanbao WebSocket communication, handling connection framing, business payloads, authentication, and group operations without external libraries.
- `gateway/platforms/yuanbao_sticker.py` — Implements Yuanbao platform sticker (TIMFaceElem) support, including a built-in catalogue, fuzzy search, and wire-format message construction.
- `gateway/restart.py` — Defines constants and parsing utilities for gateway restart behavior, including the service restart exit code and drain timeout configuration.
- `gateway/runtime_footer.py` — Renders a compact runtime-metadata footer (model, context usage, CWD) appended to the final agent response, with per-platform configuration overrides.
- `gateway/session_context.py` — Replaces process-global os.environ session state with contextvars.ContextVar to ensure concurrency-safe, task-local session tracking in the asyncio gateway.
- `gateway/session.py` — Core session management module handling context tracking, SQLite/JSONL persistence, reset policies, PII redaction, and dynamic system prompt injection.
- `gateway/shutdown_forensics.py` — Captures diagnostic context during gateway shutdown signals (SIGTERM/SIGINT) via fast synchronous snapshots and detached async subprocess probes.
- `gateway/slash_access.py` — Implements per-platform slash command access control, distinguishing between admin users and regular users across DM and group scopes.
- `gateway/status.py` — Gateway runtime status and process lifecycle management. Provides PID-file tracking, cross-platform file locking, scoped identity locks, and takeover/planned-stop markers for graceful gateway handoffs and shutdowns.
- `gateway/sticker_cache.py` — Persistent JSON cache for Telegram sticker descriptions. Stores vision-generated descriptions keyed by `file_unique_id` to avoid re-analyzing identical stickers on every message.
- `gateway/stream_consumer.py` — Async streaming consumer that bridges synchronous agent token callbacks to progressive platform message delivery. Handles edit-based streaming, native draft frames, flood-control backoff, think-block filtering, and overflow chunking.
- `gateway/whatsapp_identity.py` — Canonicalization helpers for WhatsApp sender identities. Resolves JID/LID aliases via bridge mapping files to ensure stable session keys and authorization checks across identifier variants.

## hermes_cli

- `hermes_cli/__init__.py` — Hermes CLI package initialization. Exposes version metadata and applies a Windows UTF-8 stdout/stderr fix using stream replacement rather than reconfiguration.
- `hermes_cli/_parser.py` — Defines the top-level argparse configuration and chat subparser for the Hermes CLI, centralizing flag registration and relaunch inheritance metadata.
- `hermes_cli/_subprocess_compat.py` — Provides cross-platform subprocess helpers to safely launch detached or hidden processes, with special handling for Windows batch shims and console flashes.
- `hermes_cli/auth_commands.py` — Implements CLI subcommands for managing the credential pool, supporting API key injection, OAuth flows, and interactive credential management.
- `hermes_cli/azure_detect.py` — Auto-detects Azure AI Foundry endpoint capabilities by probing for OpenAI vs Anthropic API modes and extracting available model catalogs.
- `hermes_cli/backup.py` — Manages full and quick state backups of the Hermes home directory, handling SQLite consistency, exclusion rules, and pre-update safety snapshots.
- `hermes_cli/banner.py` — Renders the Hermes startup banner, displaying model info, available tools/skills, MCP status, and non-blocking update checks.
- `hermes_cli/browser_connect.py` — Provides utilities for discovering and attaching to a locally running Chrome browser instance via the Chrome DevTools Protocol (CDP), enabling browser automation tools within the Hermes agent loop.
- `hermes_cli/callbacks.py` — Implements interactive prompt callbacks that bridge `terminal_tool`'s interactive requests (clarification, sudo, approval) into the `prompt_toolkit` TUI event loop.
- `hermes_cli/commands.py` — Central registry and dispatcher for Hermes slash commands, defining metadata, aliases, and platform-specific surfacing logic for CLI, Telegram, Discord, and Slack. Handles config-gated exposure, plugin/skill integration, and TUI autocomplete.
- `hermes_cli/completion.py` — Generates shell completion scripts for bash, zsh, and fish by introspecting the live argparse parser tree, ensuring completions dynamically reflect CLI subcommands and flags without hardcoded lists.
- `hermes_cli/copilot_auth.py` — Manages GitHub Copilot authentication, including OAuth device code flow, token validation, exchange to short-lived API tokens, and construction of standard request headers for Copilot endpoints.
- `hermes_cli/cron.py` — CLI interface for managing scheduled cron jobs, wrapping internal cron tools to handle job creation, editing, scheduling, and status reporting while checking gateway availability for automatic execution.
- `hermes_cli/curator.py` — CLI subcommand module for the skill curator system. Provides status reporting, manual review runs, pause/resume toggles, skill pinning/unpinning, archiving, bulk pruning, and backup/rollback operations. Delegates core logic to agent.curator, tools.skill_usage, and agent.curator_backup.
- `hermes_cli/curses_ui.py` — Shared curses-based terminal UI components for interactive multi-select checklists, radio lists, and single-select menus. Includes a `flush_stdin()` utility to prevent escape-sequence corruption after curses exits, plus text-based numbered fallbacks for non-curses terminals.
- `hermes_cli/debug.py` — Debug report collection and upload tool. Gathers system info and log tails, optionally redacts secrets via agent.redact, uploads to paste.rs/dpaste.com, and schedules auto-deletion via a pending.json manifest swept by the gateway cron ticker.
- `hermes_cli/default_soul.py` — Contains the default SOUL.md template string seeded into HERMES_HOME on first run. Defines Hermes Agent's core identity, communication style, and behavioral priorities.
- `hermes_cli/dingtalk_auth.py` — DingTalk Device Flow authorization module. Implements a 3-step registration (init → begin → poll) to obtain client credentials via QR code scanning, with auto-install of the qrcode library and terminal rendering.
- `hermes_cli/doctor.py` — Comprehensive diagnostic command that validates Hermes Agent setup, checking Python version, packages, config structure, auth providers, directory layout, external tools, API connectivity, and tool availability. Supports auto-fixing issues and acknowledging security advisories.
- `hermes_cli/dump.py` — CLI command that outputs a compact, plain-text summary of the Hermes setup for support contexts. Reports version, OS, SDK versions, model/provider config, API key status, feature flags, gateway state, and non-default config overrides.
- `hermes_cli/env_loader.py` — Utility module for consistently loading Hermes `.env` files across entrypoints. Handles UTF-8/Latin-1 fallbacks, pre-sanitizes corrupted lines, strips non-ASCII characters from credential variables, and manages user vs. project environment precedence.
- `hermes_cli/fallback_cmd.py` — CLI command for managing the fallback provider chain. Supports listing, adding, removing, and clearing fallback entries. Migrates legacy config formats, prevents duplicate/self-fallbacks, and safely restores model/auth state after interactive picker usage.
- `hermes_cli/gateway_windows.py` — Windows-specific backend for managing the Hermes gateway as a background service. Supports installation via Windows Scheduled Tasks (`schtasks`) with a fallback to the Startup folder, and handles starting, stopping, restarting, and status reporting.
- `hermes_cli/goals.py` — Implements the persistent session goal loop ("Ralph loop"), allowing users to set standing objectives that the agent pursues across multiple turns until satisfied, budget exhausted, or paused.
- `hermes_cli/hooks.py` — CLI interface for inspecting, testing, revoking, and diagnosing user-defined shell-script hooks that trigger on agent lifecycle events.
- `hermes_cli/inventory.py` — Centralized substrate for building provider and model inventory payloads, unifying configuration parsing and authentication state for the dashboard and TUI.
- `hermes_cli/kanban_diagnostics.py` — Stateless diagnostic engine for the Kanban board that analyzes task events and runs to surface actionable distress signals like hallucinated cards, crash loops, or stranded tasks.
- `hermes_cli/kanban_specify.py` — Triage task specifier that uses an auxiliary LLM to expand rough Kanban triage items into concrete, actionable task specifications before promotion to the `todo` column.
- `hermes_cli/kanban.py` — CLI interface for the Hermes Kanban board, exposing task management, board scoping, diagnostics, and dispatcher controls via argparse. Delegates persistence to kanban_db and integrates with gateway status checks, LLM-based triage specification, and slash-command routing for chat interfaces.
- `hermes_cli/logs.py` — CLI utility for viewing, filtering, and tailing Hermes log files under ~/.hermes/logs/. Supports level, session, component, and relative time filtering, with efficient chunked reading for large files and real-time follow mode.
- `hermes_cli/mcp_config.py` — CLI subcommand for managing MCP servers (add, remove, list, test, configure, login). Handles transport configuration, OAuth/API key setup, and interactive tool selection via curses. Relies on tools.mcp_tool for live probing and stores state in config.yaml and .env.
- `hermes_cli/memory_setup.py` — Interactive wizard for configuring memory provider plugins. Discovers providers via the plugin system, installs missing pip dependencies using uv, prompts for schema-defined fields, and writes configuration to config.yaml and .env.
- `hermes_cli/model_catalog.py` — Fetches, validates, and caches a remote JSON manifest of curated LLM models. Implements multi-tier caching (in-process, disk, network) with TTL invalidation. Provides accessors for OpenRouter and Nous Portal models, falling back to in-repo defaults on failure.
- `hermes_cli/model_normalize.py` — Centralizes provider-specific model ID translation. Maps bare names to vendor/model slugs for aggregators, handles dot/hyphen conversions for Anthropic, preserves dots for Copilot, and applies canonical routing for DeepSeek.
- `hermes_cli/model_switch.py` — Core pipeline for switching LLM models and providers across the CLI and gateway /model commands.
- `hermes_cli/nous_subscription.py` — Manages feature availability, credential routing, and interactive setup for Nous subscription managed tools.
- `hermes_cli/oneshot.py` — Implements non-interactive `-z` oneshot mode for single-prompt execution with suppressed output.
- `hermes_cli/pairing.py` — CLI interface for managing DM pairing requests, allowing operators to list, approve, revoke, or clear pending user pairings across supported platforms.
- `hermes_cli/platforms.py` — Central registry defining platform metadata and display labels, merging static built-in platforms with dynamically registered plugin platforms.
- `hermes_cli/plugins_cmd.py` — Comprehensive CLI and dashboard API for installing, updating, enabling, and configuring Hermes plugins from Git repositories.
- `hermes_cli/plugins.py` — Central plugin management module that discovers, loads, and invokes plugins from bundled, user, project, and pip sources. It provides a PluginContext for registering tools, hooks, CLI/slash commands, skills, and specialized providers, while enforcing opt-in enablement and lifecycle isolation.
- `hermes_cli/profile_distribution.py` — Handles installation, updating, and introspection of shareable Hermes profiles packaged as git repositories. It stages sources, validates manifests, and carefully separates distribution-owned configuration from user-owned state to preserve credentials and runtime data during updates.
- `hermes_cli/profiles.py` — Manages isolated Hermes profiles, each backed by a distinct HERMES_HOME directory containing independent configuration, environment variables, memory, and session state.
- `hermes_cli/runtime_provider.py` — Centralized runtime provider resolution for CLI, gateway, and cron. Orchestrates credential pooling, API mode detection, base URL routing, and provider-specific fallbacks across OpenRouter, Azure, Bedrock, Anthropic, and custom endpoints.
- `hermes_cli/security_advisories.py` — Supply-chain security scanner that checks installed packages against a catalog of known-compromised versions, surfaces remediation steps, and manages user acknowledgments via config and a local cache.
- `hermes_cli/skills_config.py` — CLI interface for toggling agent skills globally or per-platform, persisting disabled lists to config.yaml and providing category-based bulk toggling via curses UI.
- `hermes_cli/skills_hub.py` — Unified CLI and slash-command interface for managing Hermes skills, supporting search, installation, inspection, auditing, updates, and publishing.
- `hermes_cli/skin_engine.py` — Data-driven theming engine that loads built-in and user-defined YAML skins to customize CLI colors, branding, and prompt_toolkit styles.
- `hermes_cli/slack_cli.py` — CLI subcommand generator for Slack app manifests, synchronizing Hermes slash commands with Slack's native configuration format.
- `hermes_cli/status.py` — CLI status command that reports the health and configuration of all Hermes Agent components, including environment, API keys, OAuth providers, terminal backends, messaging platforms, gateway service, cron jobs, and sessions.
- `hermes_cli/stdio.py` — Windows-safe stdio configuration module that forces UTF-8 encoding on Windows consoles, sets default editor, and augments PATH with Hermes-managed tool directories to fix first-launch resolution gaps.
- `hermes_cli/timeouts.py` — Configuration helpers that extract provider-specific request and stale timeouts from config.yaml, supporting both provider-level and model-level overrides.
- `hermes_cli/tips.py` — Large corpus of random one-liner tips covering slash commands, CLI flags, configuration, tools, profiles, sessions, memory, skills, cron, voice, gateway, security, and advanced power-user features, displayed at CLI session start.
- `hermes_cli/uninstall.py` — Hermes Agent uninstaller that removes code, shell PATH entries, wrapper scripts, gateway services, Windows registry env vars, and optionally all user data, with support for named profile cleanup.
- `hermes_cli/vercel_auth.py` — Helper module that reports Vercel Sandbox authentication state by checking for OIDC tokens or access token triples, returning a structured status without exposing secret values.
- `hermes_cli/voice.py` — Process-wide voice recording and TTS API for the TUI gateway, wrapping `tools.voice_mode` and `tools.tts_tool`. Supports push-to-talk and continuous VAD modes, with a TTS-vs-STT feedback guard to prevent audio loops.
- `hermes_cli/webhook.py` — CLI subcommand for managing dynamic webhook subscriptions, persisting routes to `webhook_subscriptions.json` and supporting hot-reloading by the gateway adapter.

## optional-skills

- `optional-skills/blockchain/evm/scripts/evm_client.py` — Zero-dependency EVM blockchain CLI tool providing multi-chain balance checks, transaction decoding, gas estimation, and portfolio tracking. Uses stdlib HTTP requests, custom Keccak-256 for ABI encoding, and CoinGecko for pricing.
- `optional-skills/blockchain/hyperliquid/scripts/hyperliquid_client.py` — Zero-dependency CLI tool for querying Hyperliquid perpetual and spot markets, account states, and trade history. Features custom .env loading, formatted table rendering, and post-trade PnL review analytics.
- `optional-skills/blockchain/solana/scripts/solana_client.py` — A standalone CLI tool for querying Solana blockchain data and CoinGecko prices using only the Python standard library. It supports commands for network stats, wallet balances, transaction details, token metadata, recent activity, NFT portfolios, whale detection, and price lookups.
- `optional-skills/creative/kanban-video-orchestrator/scripts/bootstrap_pipeline.py` — A pipeline bootstrap script that reads a `plan.json` describing a video production team and generates a `setup.sh` script to configure Hermes profiles, write SOUL.md files, and initialize a Kanban task graph.
- `optional-skills/creative/kanban-video-orchestrator/scripts/monitor.py` — A polling monitor for running Kanban video pipelines that detects stuck tasks, overtime execution, and repeated retries by querying the `hermes kanban` CLI.
- `optional-skills/creative/meme-generation/scripts/generate_meme.py` — A meme generation CLI that overlays text on template images using Pillow, supporting curated templates, Imgflip API lookups, and custom image inputs with automatic font scaling and text wrapping.
- `optional-skills/devops/watchers/scripts/_watermark.py` — A shared state management module for watcher scripts that tracks seen item IDs to prevent duplicate emissions across cron runs, using atomic JSON file writes.
- `optional-skills/devops/watchers/scripts/watch_github.py` — A GitHub activity watcher that polls issues, PRs, releases, or commits via the REST API, deduplicates using the watermark system, and outputs new items as Markdown.
- `optional-skills/devops/watchers/scripts/watch_http_json.py` — A generic JSON endpoint watcher that polls any REST API, extracts items via dotted path navigation, deduplicates by a configurable ID field, and renders Markdown output.
- `optional-skills/devops/watchers/scripts/watch_rss.py` — An RSS/Atom feed watcher that parses XML feeds, extracts entries, deduplicates using the watermark system, and outputs new items as Markdown for cron delivery.
- `optional-skills/finance/dcf-model/scripts/validate_dcf.py` — A DCF Excel model validator that checks for formula errors, verifies terminal growth < WACC, validates WACC ranges, and assesses terminal value proportions using openpyxl.
- `optional-skills/finance/excel-author/scripts/recalc.py` — A utility script that recalculates Excel formulas in `.xlsx` files using headless LibreOffice, solving the problem of openpyxl writing formulas without computing values.
- `optional-skills/finance/stocks/scripts/stocks_client.py` — Stdlib-only CLI for fetching stock and crypto market data via Yahoo Finance and Alpha Vantage. Provides commands for quotes, search, history, comparison, and crypto prices, handling session cookies and exponential backoff.
- `optional-skills/health/fitness-nutrition/scripts/body_calc.py` — Standalone fitness calculator CLI computing BMI, TDEE, one-rep max, macros, and body fat percentage using standard formulas. Outputs human-readable text for direct user consumption or agent relay.
- `optional-skills/health/fitness-nutrition/scripts/nutrition_search.py` — CLI tool querying the USDA FoodData Central API for nutritional information per 100g. Supports batch queries via CLI args or stdin, falling back to a demo key if `USDA_API_KEY` is unset.
- `optional-skills/mcp/fastmcp/scripts/scaffold_fastmcp.py` — Template scaffolding script that copies FastMCP server boilerplate into a target file, replacing a `__SERVER_NAME__` placeholder. Supports listing available templates and force-overwriting existing outputs.
- `optional-skills/mcp/fastmcp/templates/api_wrapper.py` — FastMCP server template for wrapping external REST APIs. Defines tools for health checks, resource retrieval, and searching, configured via environment variables for base URL, token, and timeout.
- `optional-skills/mcp/fastmcp/templates/database_server.py` — Read-only SQLite MCP server template. Provides tools to list tables, describe schemas, and execute parameterized SELECT queries with strict mutation rejection and row limits.
- `optional-skills/mcp/fastmcp/templates/file_processor.py` — MCP server template for local text file operations. Offers tools to summarize files, search for text matches, and expose file contents as MCP resources.
- `optional-skills/mlops/training/trl-fine-tuning/templates/basic_grpo_training.py` — Production-ready GRPO training template using TRL, PEFT, and Transformers. Configures LoRA, defines correctness/format reward functions, and orchestrates dataset loading and model training.
- `optional-skills/productivity/canvas/scripts/canvas_api.py` — CLI wrapper for the Canvas LMS REST API. Authenticates via personal access token and provides commands to list enrolled courses and course assignments with pagination support.
- `optional-skills/productivity/memento-flashcards/scripts/memento_cards.py` — Spaced-repetition flashcard engine and JSON storage manager. Supports adding, rating, exporting/importing, and tracking due cards, with atomic file writes and collection-based organization.
- `optional-skills/productivity/memento-flashcards/scripts/youtube_quiz.py` — Transcript fetcher for YouTube videos, intended to supply text to the agent for quiz generation. Requires `youtube-transcript-api` and outputs normalized transcript text as JSON.
- `optional-skills/productivity/telephony/scripts/telephony.py` — Comprehensive telephony CLI managing Twilio, Bland.ai, and Vapi integrations. Handles credential persistence, number provisioning, SMS/call execution, and inbound message polling using stdlib HTTP clients.
- `optional-skills/research/domain-intel/scripts/domain_intel.py` — Passive OSINT CLI tool for domain intelligence gathering using only Python stdlib. Supports subdomain discovery via crt.sh, SSL certificate inspection, WHOIS lookups, DNS record resolution, and domain availability checks. Outputs structured JSON.
- `optional-skills/research/drug-discovery/scripts/chembl_target.py` — CLI tool that searches the ChEMBL database for a biological target and retrieves top active compounds. Queries EBI's REST API for target metadata and activity data, filtering by minimum pChEMBL value.
- `optional-skills/research/drug-discovery/scripts/ro5_screen.py` — Batch Lipinski Rule-of-5 and Veber criteria screening tool using the PubChem PUG REST API. Accepts compound names via CLI args or stdin, fetches molecular properties, and reports pass/fail status.
- `optional-skills/research/gitnexus-explorer/scripts/proxy.mjs` — Zero-dependency Node.js reverse proxy for GitNexus. Serves a static production web UI from a dist directory and proxies `/api/*` requests to a backend server. Handles SPA fallback routing.
- `optional-skills/security/oss-forensics/scripts/evidence-store.py` — JSON-based evidence store manager for open-source security forensics. Supports adding, listing, verifying, querying, exporting, and summarizing evidence entries with SHA-256 integrity tracking and chain-of-custody logging.

## packaging

- `packaging/homebrew/hermes-agent.rb` — Homebrew formula for installing Hermes Agent. Creates a Python 3.14 virtualenv, installs the package via pip, and sets up wrapper scripts with environment variables for bundled and optional skills paths.

## plugins

- `plugins/__init__.py` — Empty package initializer for the Hermes plugins directory. Serves as a namespace marker for the plugin system.
- `plugins/context_engine/__init__.py` — Context engine plugin discovery system. Scans `plugins/context_engine/<name>/` directories for engines implementing the ContextEngine ABC. Supports both `register(ctx)` function and class instantiation patterns.
- `plugins/disk-cleanup/__init__.py` — Disk cleanup plugin that auto-tracks ephemeral files created by tool calls and cleans them up at session end. Wires `post_tool_call` and `on_session_end` hooks, plus a `/disk-cleanup` slash command.
- `plugins/disk-cleanup/disk_cleanup.py` — Core library for ephemeral file cleanup. Implements deterministic cleanup rules by category (test, temp, cron-output, research, chrome-profile), atomic JSON tracking, and path safety checks scoped to HERMES_HOME.
- `plugins/example-dashboard/dashboard/plugin_api.py` — Minimal example dashboard plugin providing a single FastAPI GET endpoint at `/api/plugins/example/hello`. Exists primarily as a stable test fixture for verifying plugin API route authentication.
- `plugins/google_meet/__init__.py` — Google Meet plugin registration module. Registers tools for joining, transcribing, and leaving Meet calls via headless Chromium. Includes `on_session_end` cleanup hook and CLI command registration.
- `plugins/google_meet/audio_bridge.py` — Virtual audio bridge for feeding generated speech into Chrome's microphone input. Provisions platform-specific virtual audio devices (PulseAudio null-sink on Linux, BlackHole on macOS) for the Meet bot's Chromium instance.
- `plugins/google_meet/cli.py` — CLI command handlers for the Google Meet plugin. Implements `hermes meet` subcommands for setup, install, auth, join, status, transcript, say, stop, and remote node management.
- `plugins/google_meet/meet_bot.py` — Headless Playwright bot for Google Meet that scrapes live captions via DOM observers and supports v2 realtime agent speech. Handles lobby admission, duration limits, and graceful shutdown while routing synthesized audio through virtual audio bridges.
- `plugins/google_meet/node/__init__.py` — Package initializer for the remote node subsystem. Re-exports core RPC classes and protocol utilities to enable JSON-over-WebSocket communication between the Hermes gateway and remote bot hosts.
- `plugins/google_meet/node/cli.py` — CLI dispatcher for `hermes meet node` subcommands. Handles node server startup, registry management, and health checks by delegating to the underlying RPC and registry modules.
- `plugins/google_meet/node/client.py` — Synchronous WebSocket RPC client for remote meet nodes. Opens short-lived connections per request, handles envelope encoding/decoding, and exposes convenience methods for bot control and speech queuing.
- `plugins/google_meet/node/protocol.py` — Wire protocol definitions for gateway-node communication. Defines JSON envelope structures, request/response/error construction, serialization helpers, and token-based request validation.
- `plugins/google_meet/node/registry.py` — File-backed JSON registry for approved remote nodes. Manages `nodes.json` persistence, providing APIs to add, remove, list, and resolve node entries by name or auto-selection.
- `plugins/google_meet/node/server.py` — WebSocket server for remote bot hosts. Validates incoming RPC requests against a persisted token, dispatches commands to `process_manager`, and directly writes speech requests to the bot's JSONL queue.
- `plugins/google_meet/process_manager.py` — Subprocess lifecycle manager for the Meet bot. Spawns detached `meet_bot.py` instances, tracks active sessions in `.active.json`, and provides local APIs for status checks, transcript reading, speech queueing, and graceful termination.
- `plugins/google_meet/realtime/__init__.py` — Package initializer for the realtime speech subpackage. Exports `RealtimeSession` and `RealtimeSpeaker` classes to facilitate OpenAI Realtime API integration and file-queue audio playback.
- `plugins/google_meet/realtime/openai_client.py` — OpenAI Realtime API WebSocket client and queue-driven speaker. Manages session setup, text-to-audio synthesis, PCM file writing, barge-in cancellation, and polls a JSONL queue to sequentially process speech requests.
- `plugins/google_meet/tools.py` — Agent-facing tool handlers for Google Meet integration. Defines schemas and routing logic for `meet_join`, `meet_status`, `meet_transcript`, `meet_leave`, and `meet_say`, delegating to local or remote execution paths.
- `plugins/hermes-achievements/dashboard/plugin_api.py` — FastAPI backend for the Hermes Achievements dashboard plugin. Scans session history, extracts metrics via regex heuristics, evaluates tiered and multi-condition achievements, and serves cached results through REST endpoints.
- `plugins/hermes-achievements/tests/test_achievement_engine.py` — Unit test suite validating the Hermes Achievements engine's message analysis, tier evaluation, secret achievement hiding, multi-condition unlocking, and model/provider aggregation logic.
- `plugins/image_gen/openai-codex/__init__.py` — Image generation provider routing `gpt-image-2` requests through the ChatGPT/Codex OAuth backend instead of a standard API key.
- `plugins/image_gen/openai/__init__.py` — Standard OpenAI image generation provider using `OPENAI_API_KEY` to call `images.generate` for `gpt-image-2` at low/medium/high quality tiers.
- `plugins/image_gen/xai/__init__.py` — xAI image generation provider using `XAI_API_KEY` to call `grok-imagine-image` with configurable aspect ratios and resolutions.
- `plugins/kanban/dashboard/plugin_api.py` — FastAPI router exposing REST and WebSocket endpoints for the Kanban dashboard, delegating all persistence to hermes_cli.kanban_db.
- `plugins/memory/__init__.py` — Plugin discovery and loader for memory providers, scanning bundled and user-installed directories with precedence rules and dynamic import handling.
- `plugins/memory/byterover/__init__.py` — ByteRover memory provider plugin wrapping the brv CLI to offer persistent, hierarchical knowledge storage and retrieval.
- `plugins/memory/hindsight/__init__.py` — Implements the Hindsight long-term memory provider, supporting cloud and local modes with knowledge graph retrieval, entity resolution, and multi-strategy recall. Manages async operations via a shared background event loop and serializes retains through a dedicated writer thread.
- `plugins/memory/holographic/__init__.py` — Implements the Holographic memory provider using SQLite-backed structured facts, entity resolution, and HRR-based compositional retrieval. Features trust scoring, auto-extraction from conversations, and mirroring of built-in memory writes.
- `plugins/memory/holographic/holographic.py` — Provides the mathematical foundation for Holographic Reduced Representations using phase vectors. Enables deterministic encoding, binding, bundling, and similarity operations with graceful numpy dependency handling.
- `plugins/memory/holographic/retrieval.py` — Implements FactRetriever, a hybrid search engine combining SQLite FTS5, Jaccard similarity, and Holographic Reduced Representation (HRR) vectors for trust-weighted memory retrieval.
- `plugins/memory/holographic/store.py` — Provides MemoryStore, a thread-safe SQLite backend for facts, entities, and HRR memory banks, featuring FTS5 indexing, entity resolution, and trust scoring.
- `plugins/memory/honcho/__init__.py` — Implements HonchoMemoryProvider, integrating Honcho AI for cross-session user modeling, dialectic reasoning, and semantic search via a pluggable memory interface.
- `plugins/memory/honcho/cli.py` — CLI interface for managing Honcho memory integration across Hermes profiles. Provides interactive setup, profile cloning, status inspection, session mapping, peer identity management, and OpenClaw migration workflows. Reads and writes honcho.json configuration files with host-block isolation per profile.
- `plugins/memory/honcho/client.py` — Configuration resolution and client initialization for the Honcho memory backend. Implements a 3-tier config file fallback, host-specific setting inheritance, session name resolution strategies, and lazy SDK loading. Manages the Honcho client singleton with local/cloud deployment detection.
- `plugins/memory/honcho/session.py` — Manages conversation sessions backed by the Honcho AI-native memory system, providing local caching, async message syncing, dialectic querying, and migration of legacy local memory files.
- `plugins/memory/mem0/__init__.py` — Implements the `MemoryProvider` interface for the Mem0 Platform API, offering server-side fact extraction, semantic search, and automatic deduplication with circuit breaker protection.
- `plugins/memory/openviking/__init__.py` — Implements the `MemoryProvider` interface for OpenViking, a context database organizing knowledge into a filesystem hierarchy with tiered loading, semantic search, and resource ingestion.
- `plugins/memory/retaindb/__init__.py` — RetainDB cloud memory plugin implementing the MemoryProvider interface. Provides semantic search, user profile retrieval, dialectic synthesis, agent self-modeling from SOUL.md, and a shared file store. Uses a durable SQLite write-behind queue for crash-safe async session ingest.
- `plugins/memory/supermemory/__init__.py` — Supermemory memory plugin implementing the MemoryProvider interface. Provides semantic long-term memory with profile recall, multi-container support, explicit memory tools, cleaned turn capture, and session-end conversation ingest via the Supermemory Python SDK.
- `plugins/model-providers/ai-gateway/__init__.py` — Vercel AI Gateway provider profile. Routes to multiple backends with attribution headers and reasoning config passthrough in the request body.
- `plugins/model-providers/alibaba-coding-plan/__init__.py` — Alibaba Cloud Coding Plan provider profile. Separate from standard Alibaba because it targets a dedicated coding-tier endpoint with its own API key.
- `plugins/model-providers/alibaba/__init__.py` — Alibaba Cloud DashScope provider profile. Standard international endpoint for Qwen and other Alibaba models.
- `plugins/model-providers/anthropic/__init__.py` — Native Anthropic provider profile. Overrides fetch_models to use Anthropic's x-api-key header and anthropic-version header for model listing.
- `plugins/model-providers/arcee/__init__.py` — Arcee AI provider profile. Simple registration with standard OpenAI-compatible endpoint.
- `plugins/model-providers/azure-foundry/__init__.py` — Azure AI Foundry provider profile. OpenAI-compatible endpoint where users supply their own per-resource base URL at setup.
- `plugins/model-providers/bedrock/__init__.py` — AWS Bedrock provider profile. Uses AWS SDK for authentication and model listing, not REST endpoints.
- `plugins/model-providers/copilot-acp/__init__.py` — GitHub Copilot ACP provider profile. Represents an external ACP subprocess rather than a standard REST transport.
- `plugins/model-providers/copilot/__init__.py` — GitHub Copilot/Models provider profile. Adds editor attribution headers and model-catalog-gated reasoning effort normalization for chat_completions models.
- `plugins/model-providers/custom/__init__.py` — Custom/Ollama local provider profile. Supports Ollama-specific options like num_ctx and think=false for disabling reasoning.
- `plugins/model-providers/deepseek/__init__.py` — DeepSeek provider profile. Standard OpenAI-compatible endpoint with fallback model list.
- `plugins/model-providers/gemini/__init__.py` — Google Gemini provider profiles. Translates reasoning_config to thinking_config in extra_body, with separate handling for native and OpenAI-compat endpoints.
- `plugins/model-providers/gmi/__init__.py` — GMI Cloud provider profile. Multi-model direct API with slash-form model IDs and Hermes attribution headers.
- `plugins/model-providers/huggingface/__init__.py` — HuggingFace Inference API provider profile. Standard OpenAI-compatible endpoint with fallback models.
- `plugins/model-providers/kilocode/__init__.py` — Kilo Code provider profile. Gateway endpoint with Gemini default aux model.
- `plugins/model-providers/kimi-coding/__init__.py` — Kimi/Moonshot provider profiles. Translates reasoning_config to Kimi-specific thinking and reasoning_effort parameters, with separate international and China endpoints.
- `plugins/model-providers/minimax/__init__.py` — MiniMax provider profiles (international, China, OAuth). All use anthropic_messages api_mode via /anthropic endpoint paths.
- `plugins/model-providers/nous/__init__.py` — Nous Portal provider profile. Injects portal attribution tags and handles reasoning config with Nous-specific omission when disabled.
- `plugins/model-providers/novita/__init__.py` — NovitaAI provider profile. Pay-per-use endpoint with extensive fallback model list.
- `plugins/model-providers/nvidia/__init__.py` — NVIDIA NIM provider profile. Accelerated inference endpoint with Llama-based fallback models.
- `plugins/model-providers/ollama-cloud/__init__.py` — Ollama Cloud provider profile. Cloud-hosted Ollama endpoint with Nemotron default aux model.
- `plugins/model-providers/openai-codex/__init__.py` — OpenAI Codex (Responses API) provider profile. Uses OAuth external auth and codex_responses api_mode.
- `plugins/model-providers/opencode-zen/__init__.py` — OpenCode provider profiles (Zen + Go). Two tiers with different model routing and default aux models.
- `plugins/model-providers/openrouter/__init__.py` — A minimal package initializer for the OpenRouter model provider plugin, currently containing only a module-level docstring.
- `plugins/platforms/line/adapter.py` — LINE Messaging API platform adapter implementing an aiohttp webhook server, signature verification, markdown stripping, and a slow-LLM postback button state machine. Handles media serving with path traversal guards and provides standalone cron delivery.
- `plugins/platforms/teams/__init__.py` — Package initializer for the Microsoft Teams platform plugin. Re-exports the `register` function from `adapter.py` to satisfy the Hermes plugin discovery system.
- `plugins/platforms/teams/adapter.py` — Microsoft Teams platform adapter bridging the `microsoft-teams-apps` SDK to an aiohttp webhook server. Handles message processing, Adaptive Card approvals, meeting summary delivery, and standalone cron dispatch with strict security validation.
- `plugins/spotify/__init__.py` — Spotify integration plugin that registers seven playback, search, and library management tools into the `spotify` toolset, gated by runtime credential checks.
- `plugins/spotify/client.py` — Thin HTTP client for the Spotify Web API. Handles credential resolution, request execution with automatic 401 retry, structured error shaping, and playback/search/playlist/library operations. Includes helpers for normalizing Spotify URIs and IDs.
- `plugins/spotify/tools.py` — Hermes tool definitions for Spotify integration. Maps agent tool calls to `SpotifyClient` methods, handles argument coercion, validates inputs, and defines JSON schemas for playback, devices, queue, search, playlists, albums, and library management.
- `plugins/teams_pipeline/__init__.py` — Plugin entry point for the Teams meeting pipeline. Registers the `teams-pipeline` CLI command for operator-facing management, explicitly avoiding model tool registration to keep agent interactions terminal-based.
- `plugins/teams_pipeline/cli.py` — CLI interface for managing the Teams pipeline. Provides commands to list/show/replay jobs, fetch meeting artifacts, manage Microsoft Graph subscriptions, inspect token health, and validate pipeline configuration against gateway settings.
- `plugins/teams_pipeline/meetings.py` — Graph API helpers for Teams meetings. Resolves meeting references, lists and downloads transcripts and recordings, fetches call records, and normalizes raw Graph payloads into structured `MeetingArtifact` objects with robust error handling.
- `plugins/teams_pipeline/models.py` — Data models for the Teams pipeline. Defines `GraphSubscription`, `TeamsMeetingRef`, `MeetingArtifact`, `TeamsMeetingSummaryPayload`, and `TeamsMeetingPipelineJob` with validation, serialization, and deserialization logic.
- `plugins/teams_pipeline/pipeline.py` — Core orchestration for Teams meeting summaries. Manages job lifecycle, resolves meetings, fetches transcripts or falls back to recording STT, generates LLM/heuristic summaries, and writes results to Notion, Linear, and Teams sinks.
- `plugins/teams_pipeline/runtime.py` — Gateway runtime wiring for the Teams pipeline. Builds configuration from gateway settings, instantiates the pipeline, lazily imports the Teams sender, and binds the notification scheduler to the `msgraph_webhook` adapter.
- `plugins/teams_pipeline/store.py` — JSON-backed durable store for Teams pipeline state. Manages subscriptions, notification receipts, event timestamps, jobs, and sink records using atomic file writes and threading locks for concurrency safety.
- `plugins/teams_pipeline/subscriptions.py` — Microsoft Graph subscription lifecycle manager. Syncs remote subscriptions with the local store, validates expiration, and renews managed subscriptions via `MicrosoftGraphClient` while tracking renewal status.
- `plugins/video_gen/fal/__init__.py` — FAL.ai video generation backend plugin. Routes requests to model families (LTX, Pixverse, Veo, etc.) for text-to-video or image-to-video. Handles payload construction, capability gating, and lazy `fal_client` loading.
- `plugins/video_gen/xai/__init__.py` — xAI Grok-Imagine video generation backend. Supports text-to-video and image-to-video via async HTTP polling. Manages reference images, duration clamping, and creates a temporary event loop for sync-to-async bridging.
- `plugins/web/__init__.py` — Package marker for bundled web search providers. Documents the auto-loading convention where subdirectories register backends via `ctx.register_web_search_provider()` into the agent registry.
- `plugins/web/brave_free/__init__.py` — Entry point for the Brave Search (free tier) plugin. Instantiates and registers `BraveFreeWebSearchProvider` with the plugin context.
- `plugins/web/brave_free/provider.py` — Brave Search free-tier provider implementation. Queries the Brave API via `httpx`, normalizes results into a standard schema, and handles rate limits, missing keys, and HTTP errors.
- `plugins/web/ddgs/__init__.py` — Entry point for the DuckDuckGo search plugin. Registers `DDGSWebSearchProvider`, which relies on the optional `ddgs` Python package for HTML scraping.
- `plugins/web/ddgs/provider.py` — DuckDuckGo search provider implementation. Uses the `ddgs` package to scrape results, handles missing dependencies gracefully, and normalizes hits into the standard web search schema.
- `plugins/web/exa/__init__.py` — Entry point for the Exa web search + extract plugin. Registers `ExaWebSearchProvider`, backed by the official `exa-py` SDK with lazy loading.
- `plugins/web/exa/provider.py` — Exa search + extract provider. Lazy-loads the SDK, caches the client on `tools.web_tools` for test compatibility, and implements sync search/extract with interrupt checks and error normalization.
- `plugins/web/firecrawl/__init__.py` — Entry point for the Firecrawl web search + extract plugin. Registers `FirecrawlWebSearchProvider`, supporting both direct API and managed gateway authentication paths.
- `plugins/web/firecrawl/provider.py` — Firecrawl provider implementation. Handles lazy SDK proxying, dual auth resolution, async extract/crawl with 60s timeouts, SSRF re-checks post-redirect, and cross-SDK response normalization.
- `plugins/web/parallel/__init__.py` — Entry point for the Parallel.ai web search + extract plugin. Registers `ParallelWebSearchProvider`, notable for being the first plugin to expose an async `extract()` method.
- `plugins/web/parallel/provider.py` — Parallel.ai provider implementation. Uses separate sync and async SDK clients, caches them on `tools.web_tools`, supports configurable search modes, and normalizes extraction results with per-URL error handling.
- `plugins/web/searxng/__init__.py` — Entry point for the SearXNG search plugin. Registers `SearXNGWebSearchProvider` for querying user-hosted SearXNG instances.
- `plugins/web/searxng/provider.py` — SearXNG provider implementation. Queries the `/search?format=json` endpoint, sorts results by score, and normalizes them. Search-only, requiring pairing with an extract provider.
- `plugins/web/tavily/__init__.py` — Entry point for the Tavily web search + extract + crawl plugin. Registers `TavilyWebSearchProvider`, the first plugin to advertise `supports_crawl=True` for seed URL crawling.
- `plugins/web/tavily/provider.py` — Tavily web search plugin implementing search, extract, and crawl capabilities via synchronous httpx POST calls. Handles dual auth modes (body-only for search/extract, Bearer header for crawl) and normalizes responses into a standard document shape.

## providers
