- `providers/__init__.py` — Lazy provider profile registry that discovers plugins from bundled and user directories, plus legacy single-file modules. User plugins override bundled ones on name collision via last-writer-wins semantics.
- `providers/base.py` — Declarative `ProviderProfile` dataclass defining provider metadata, auth, endpoints, model catalog, client quirks, and request-level hooks. Profiles describe behavior without owning client construction or streaming.

## scripts

- `scripts/benchmark_browser_eval.py` — Manual benchmark comparing supervisor WebSocket Runtime.evaluate path against agent-browser subprocess eval path. Starts a headless Chrome instance and runs N iterations of `1 + 1` evaluation.
- `scripts/build_model_catalog.py` — Builds a centralized JSON model catalog manifest from in-repo hardcoded lists (OPENROUTER_MODELS, _PROVIDER_MODELS["nous"]). Output served as static file on docs site for runtime fetching.
- `scripts/build_skills_index.py` — Crawls multiple skill sources (skills.sh, GitHub, ClawHub, LobeHub, Claude Marketplace) and builds a deduplicated JSON index with resolved GitHub paths. Uses parallel tree lookups for efficient path resolution.
- `scripts/check-windows-footguns.py` — Grep-based static checker for Windows cross-platform footguns. Scans staged changes, diffs, or full repo for patterns like bare os.kill, missing encoding=, and POSIX-only signals.
- `scripts/contributor_audit.py` — Cross-references git authors, Co-authored-by trailers, and salvaged PR descriptions to find contributors missing from release notes. Supports strict CI mode for unmapped email detection.
- `scripts/discord-voice-doctor.py` — Diagnostic tool for Discord voice channel support. Checks Python packages, system tools, environment variables, config, and bot permissions needed for voice mode.
- `scripts/install_psutil_android.py` — Installs psutil on Termux/Android by patching upstream platform detection to include 'android' alongside 'linux'. Downloads sdist, applies one-line patch, installs with pip/uv.
- `scripts/keystroke_diagnostic.py` — Interactive prompt_toolkit app that displays how the current terminal identifies keystrokes. Shows Keys.* identifiers and raw escape bytes for debugging keybinding issues.
- `scripts/lint_diff.py` — CLI tool that diffs ruff and ty diagnostic JSON reports between two git refs, producing a Markdown summary for GitHub PR comments or step summaries. Normalizes diagnostics to a stable (path, rule, message) key so line-number shifts from unrelated edits don't create false positives.
- `scripts/profile-tui.py` — Performance profiling harness for the Hermes TUI. Launches the Node.js TUI in a PTY, simulates sustained keystroke input, parses `perf.log` for React and Ink pipeline events, and computes percentile/FPS/phase breakdowns with A/B diff support.
- `scripts/release.py` — Automated release tool for Hermes Agent. Generates CalVer tags, bumps semver, parses git history into categorized changelogs, resolves authors to GitHub @mentions, and optionally publishes via the `gh` CLI with built Python artifacts.
- `scripts/sample_and_compress.py` — Pipeline to sample trajectories from HuggingFace datasets, filter by minimum token count using parallel tokenization, run trajectory compression, and merge results into a single JSONL file for dataset publishing.
- `scripts/whatsapp-bridge/allowlist.js` — WhatsApp identifier normalization and allowlist matching module. Strips JID/LID syntax, resolves phone↔LID aliases from session mapping files, and enforces a secure-default allowlist (empty = reject all).
- `scripts/whatsapp-bridge/allowlist.test.mjs` — Unit tests for the WhatsApp allowlist module. Validates identifier normalization, LID/phone alias resolution from session mapping files, wildcard behavior, and the secure-default empty-allowlist regression guard.
- `scripts/whatsapp-bridge/bridge.js` — Standalone Node.js bridge connecting WhatsApp to the Python gateway via Baileys. Exposes HTTP endpoints for messaging, media handling, and health checks while managing QR pairing, session persistence, echo-back prevention, and DNS rebinding protection.

## skills

- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/_common.py` — Shared utility module for ComfyUI skill scripts providing HTTP transport with retry/backoff, cloud vs local endpoint routing, workflow validation helpers, model/loader catalogs, and path-traversal safeguards.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/auto_fix_deps.py` — CLI tool that verifies ComfyUI workflow dependencies and automatically installs missing custom nodes or models using `comfy-cli`, requiring explicit URLs for model downloads to prevent accidental fetches.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/check_deps.py` — Verifies a ComfyUI workflow's dependencies (custom nodes, models, embeddings) against a running server, handling cloud vs local API differences, folder aliasing, and free-tier restrictions.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/extract_schema.py` — Analyzes ComfyUI API-format workflows to extract controllable parameters, output nodes, and model/embedding dependencies, tracing sampler connections to identify prompts and resolving duplicate names.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/fetch_logs.py` — Retrieves workflow execution diagnostics and tracebacks from a ComfyUI server, supporting both local `/history` and cloud `/jobs` endpoints with structured error extraction and queue monitoring.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/hardware_check.py` — Detects local machine capabilities (GPU, RAM, disk, OS, WSL/Rosetta) to determine if ComfyUI can run locally or should fallback to cloud. Outputs a structured JSON report with verdicts and CLI flags.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/health_check.py` — Verifies ComfyUI environment readiness by checking CLI availability, server reachability, checkpoint presence, optional workflow dependencies, and an optional smoke test.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/run_batch.py` — Executes a ComfyUI workflow multiple times with varying parameters, supporting sequential or parallel runs via `ThreadPoolExecutor` and cartesian product sweeps.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/run_workflow.py` — Core workflow executor that injects parameters, submits jobs, monitors progress via HTTP polling or WebSockets, and safely downloads outputs for local or cloud ComfyUI servers.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/scripts/ws_monitor.py` — Real-time WebSocket monitor that pretty-prints ComfyUI execution events, progress, and binary preview frames to the terminal.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/tests/conftest.py` — Pytest configuration for the ComfyUI skill suite, providing shared fixtures for workflows, directories, and cloud API keys, plus auto-skipping for cloud tests.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/tests/test_check_deps.py` — Unit tests for dependency checking logic, focusing on model name normalization, presence matching, and node-to-package mapping safety.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/tests/test_cloud_integration.py` — Live integration tests against Comfy Cloud API endpoints, verifying URL routing, model parsing, dependency checking, and health check flows.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/tests/test_common.py` — Comprehensive unit tests for `_common.py` utilities, covering cloud detection, URL routing, workflow parsing, path safety, seed coercion, and redirect header stripping.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/tests/test_extract_schema.py` — Tests for workflow schema extraction, verifying connection tracing, prompt node detection, parameter mapping, embedding dependencies, and duplicate deduplication.
- `skills/creative/comfyui/tests/test_run_workflow.py` — Unit tests for `run_workflow.py` logic, covering input image parsing, parameter injection safety, output download walking, and runner construction.
- `skills/creative/excalidraw/scripts/upload.py` — Standalone CLI that encrypts and uploads .excalidraw JSON files to excalidraw.com's public v2 API, returning a shareable URL with the decryption key embedded in the URL fragment.
- `skills/creative/p5js/scripts/export-frames.js` — Puppeteer-based headless frame exporter for p5.js sketches, supporting deterministic capture via noLoop/redraw or timed fallback mode for sketches that don't signal readiness.
- `skills/creative/pixel-art/scripts/palettes.py` — Library of named RGB palettes (hardware and artistic) for pixel art generation, with a helper to build PIL-compatible palette images for quantization.
- `skills/creative/pixel-art/scripts/pixel_art_video.py` — Overlays procedural pixel animations (stars, rain, fireflies, etc.) onto a base image and encodes the result to MP4 and optionally GIF via ffmpeg.
- `skills/creative/pixel-art/scripts/pixel_art.py` — Image-to-pixel-art converter using Floyd-Steinberg dithering, supporting both adaptive palettes and named hardware/artistic palettes via configurable presets.
- `skills/media/youtube-content/scripts/fetch_transcript.py` — Fetches YouTube video transcripts via youtube-transcript-api and outputs structured JSON with segments, full text, and optional timestamps.
- `skills/productivity/google-workspace/scripts/_hermes_home.py` — Standalone HERMES_HOME resolver that mirrors hermes_constants' API, enabling Google Workspace scripts to run outside the Hermes process without import dependencies.
- `skills/productivity/google-workspace/scripts/google_api.py` — Comprehensive Google Workspace API CLI supporting Gmail, Calendar, Drive, Contacts, Sheets, and Docs — with dual execution paths via gws CLI or Python client libraries.
- `skills/productivity/google-workspace/scripts/gws_bridge.py` — Token refresh bridge that validates/refreshes the Google OAuth token, then execs the gws CLI with the access token injected as an environment variable.
- `skills/productivity/google-workspace/scripts/setup.py` — Non-interactive Google Workspace OAuth2 setup script designed for agent-driven workflows, supporting PKCE flow, scope validation, and live API health checks.
- `skills/productivity/linear/scripts/linear_api.py` — Zero-dependency Linear GraphQL API CLI using only Python stdlib, supporting issue management, document operations, and raw query execution.
- `skills/productivity/maps/scripts/maps_client.py` — Stdlib-only CLI tool for geocoding, POI search, routing, and timezone lookups using OpenStreetMap/Nominatim, Overpass API, OSRM, and TimeAPI.io. Supports search, reverse geocode, nearby POIs, distance, directions, timezone, bounding-box queries, and area lookups.
- `skills/productivity/ocr-and-documents/scripts/extract_marker.py` — High-quality document text extraction using marker-pdf with OCR and layout analysis. Supports PDF, DOCX, PPTX, XLSX, HTML, EPUB, and images. Requires ~3-5GB disk for PyTorch models. Offers optional LLM-boosted accuracy and JSON output.
- `skills/productivity/ocr-and-documents/scripts/extract_pymupdf.py` — Lightweight document text extraction using pymupdf (~25MB, no ML models). Supports plain text, markdown, tables, images, and metadata extraction from PDFs with optional page range filtering.
- `skills/productivity/powerpoint/scripts/add_slide.py` — Adds slides to unpacked PPTX directories by duplicating existing slides or creating from layout templates. Updates [Content_Types].xml, presentation.xml.rels, and prints the sldId element for manual insertion.
- `skills/productivity/powerpoint/scripts/clean.py` — Removes unreferenced files from unpacked PPTX directories: orphaned slides, trash directory, unreferenced media/embeddings/charts/diagrams/drawings/ink, orphaned .rels files, and stale Content-Type overrides.
- `skills/productivity/powerpoint/scripts/office/helpers/merge_runs.py` — Merges adjacent DOCX runs with identical formatting, removes rsid revision metadata, and strips proofErr elements. Operates on unpacked DOCX `word/document.xml` to reduce XML bloat.
- `skills/productivity/powerpoint/scripts/office/helpers/simplify_redlines.py` — Simplifies DOCX tracked changes by merging adjacent `<w:ins>` or `<w:del>` elements from the same author. Also provides author inference by comparing tracked changes between original and modified documents.
- `skills/productivity/powerpoint/scripts/office/pack.py` — Packs unpacked Office document directories into .docx/.pptx/.xlsx files with XML condensation, schema validation, and auto-repair. Supports optional validation against original files.
- `skills/red-teaming/godmode/scripts/auto_jailbreak.py` — Automated jailbreak pipeline that tests model-specific prompt injection strategies against canary queries, scores compliance, and writes winning configurations to config.yaml and prefill.json.
- `skills/red-teaming/godmode/scripts/godmode_race.py` — Multi-model racing engine that queries up to 55 LLMs via OpenRouter in parallel, scores responses on quality, refusal detection, and hedge patterns, then returns the best unfiltered answer. Includes tiered model selection and a 'classic' mode racing 5 jailbreak prompt combos.
- `skills/red-teaming/godmode/scripts/load_godmode.py` — Namespace loader that exec-compiles and merges globals from three godmode scripts (parseltongue.py, godmode_race.py, auto_jailbreak.py) into the calling scope, handling sys.argv preservation and cleanup of loader internals.
- `skills/red-teaming/godmode/scripts/parseltongue.py` — Input obfuscation engine with 33 text transformation techniques across 3 tiers (light/standard/heavy) for bypassing LLM safety classifiers. Supports leetspeak, Unicode homoglyphs, Morse, Braille, base64, and multi-layer combos.
- `skills/research/arxiv/scripts/search_arxiv.py` — CLI tool for searching arXiv papers via the export API. Supports keyword queries, author/category filters, direct ID lookups, and sorting by relevance/date/updated. Parses Atom XML responses and formats clean output.
- `skills/research/polymarket/scripts/polymarket.py` — CLI helper for querying Polymarket prediction market data across three API endpoints (Gamma, CLOB, Data). Supports market search, trending events, price/orderbook queries, price history visualization, and recent trades.

## sm-skills

- `sm-skills/security/email_parse_plugin/scripts/email_parse.py` — Email threat detection integration script that uploads .eml files to an internal analysis system, polls async task status, and retrieves structured reports. Uses a custom processData:/errorData: output protocol for agent orchestration.
- `sm-skills/security/nts/assets/nts_client.py` — Minimal CLI client for the NTS WebAPI v7.3.0. Handles system info, health, netlinks, IOC queries, and pcap downloads. Parses custom binary pcap streams into standard libpcap format. Includes alert file parsing and batch download workflows.
- `sm-skills/security/sandbox/assets/sandbox_client.py` — Minimal CLI client for the 数默科技 sandbox online-submission API. Orchestrates sample upload, MD5 deduplication, report polling, and part fetching. High-level `analyze` method chains these steps. Auth via token parameter.

## tests

- `tests/acp_adapter/test_acp_commands.py` — Tests ACP adapter slash command handling (`/steer`, `/queue`). Verifies session DB injection, steer behavior during running/idle/interrupted states, and queued prompt draining. Uses mocked agent and connection objects.
- `tests/acp_adapter/test_acp_images.py` — Tests ACP multimodal content conversion to OpenAI format. Covers text, image, resource link, and embedded blob blocks. Validates MIME inference, base64 inlining, and image capability advertisement during agent initialization.
- `tests/acp/test_approval_isolation.py` — Regression tests for ACP approval flow security fixes (GHSA-96vc, GHSA-qg5c). Verifies thread-local callback isolation, sudo password cache scoping via contextvars, and `HERMES_INTERACTIVE` routing to prevent auto-approve bypasses.
- `tests/acp/test_auth.py` — Tests `acp_adapter.auth` provider detection logic. Validates `has_provider` and `detect_provider` against mocked runtime provider resolution, handling missing keys and resolution errors gracefully.
- `tests/acp/test_entry.py` — Tests `acp_adapter.entry` startup wiring. Confirms that `use_unstable_protocol=True` is correctly passed to `acp.run_agent` during initialization.
- `tests/acp/test_events.py` — Tests `acp_adapter.events` callback factories for ACP notifications. Validates thread-safe coroutine submission, tool call ID tracking, metadata capture, and proper emission of `ToolCallStart`, `AgentThoughtChunk`, and `AgentMessageChunk` events.
- `tests/acp/test_mcp_e2e.py` — End-to-end tests for ACP MCP server registration and tool-result reporting. Covers session lifecycle (new, load, resume, fork), stdio/HTTP server conversion, tool event pairing by call ID, and patch mode diff block generation.
- `tests/acp/test_permissions.py` — Tests `acp_adapter.permissions` approval bridge. Validates async `request_permission` scheduling, option mapping (allow_once/session/always/deny), timeout handling, and proper tool call structure generation for ACP clients.
- `tests/acp/test_ping_suppression.py` — Tests `acp_adapter.entry._BenignProbeMethodFilter` and E2E ping handling. Verifies suppression of benign probe errors (ping/health) in logs, proper JSON-RPC error responses, and clean stderr output over stdio pipes.
- `tests/acp/test_server.py` — Comprehensive test suite for HermesACPAgent covering ACP protocol initialization, authentication, session lifecycle, prompt dispatch, slash command handling, usage reporting, and MCP server registration within sessions.
- `tests/acp/test_session.py` — Tests for SessionManager and SessionState covering session creation, WSL path translation, forking, persistence via SessionDB, FTS search, and provider snapshot preservation across process restarts.
- `tests/acp/test_tools.py` — Tests for ACP tool kind mapping, title building, and structured content construction for tool start/complete events, covering patch diffs, terminal output, search results, and skill management.
- `tests/agent/lsp/__init__.py` — Empty package init file marking the LSP test directory as a Python package for pytest discovery.
- `tests/agent/lsp/_mock_lsp_server.py` — Minimal in-process LSP server mock supporting configurable behaviors (clean, errors, crash, slow) via MOCK_LSP_SCRIPT env var for integration testing without external language servers.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_backend_gate.py` — Integration tests verifying the LSP layer is correctly skipped when the agent's terminal environment is non-local (Docker/Modal/SSH), preventing futile diagnostic queries.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_broken_set.py` — Tests for the LSP broken-set short-circuit mechanism that prevents repeated timeout penalties when a language server fails to initialize or hangs.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_client_e2e.py` — End-to-end integration tests driving LSPClient against the mock LSP server subprocess, validating lifecycle, diagnostic flow, crash recovery, and deduplication.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_diagnostics_field.py` — Tests for the lsp_diagnostics field on WriteResult/PatchResult, ensuring LSP semantic diagnostics and syntax lint output remain in separate channels.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_eventlog.py` — Validates the structured logging deduplication logic for LSP events, ensuring steady-state events log at DEBUG while state transitions and diagnostics surface at INFO/WARNING exactly once or per-call as designed.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_install_and_lint_fixes.py` — Validates post-fix behaviors for LSP installation recipes, backend dependency warnings, and linter error classification to prevent false negatives from blocking semantic analysis.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_lifecycle.py` — Tests the singleton lifecycle management for the LSP service, focusing on `atexit` registration, idempotent shutdown, and graceful exception handling during process termination.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_protocol.py` — Exercises the low-level LSP protocol framing layer, verifying JSON-RPC message encoding, Content-Length header parsing, partial-read tolerance, and message classification.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_reporter.py` — Validates the diagnostic formatting and reporting layer, ensuring correct 1-indexed positions, severity labels, truncation limits, and severity filtering.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_service.py` — Integration tests for the synchronous `LSPService` wrapper, verifying workspace gating, delta filtering against baselines, and status reporting using a mock LSP server.
- `tests/agent/lsp/test_workspace.py` — Tests workspace and project-root resolution logic, covering git worktree discovery, marker file prioritization, and path normalization.
- `tests/agent/test_anthropic_adapter.py` — Comprehensive test suite for agent/anthropic_adapter.py covering OAuth token resolution, client building across auth methods, credential file I/O, message/tool conversion, thinking block management, reasoning config mapping, and response normalization.
- `tests/agent/test_anthropic_keychain.py` — Tests for macOS Keychain credential support in agent/anthropic_adapter.py, verifying Darwin-only behavior, security command invocation, JSON parsing, and Keychain-over-file priority.
- `tests/agent/test_arcee_trinity_overrides.py` — Tests for Arcee Trinity Large Thinking model-specific overrides in agent/auxiliary_client.py, covering temperature pinning, compression threshold adjustment, and precise model name matching.
- `tests/agent/test_auxiliary_client_anthropic_custom.py` — Tests for agent/auxiliary_client._try_custom_endpoint's anthropic_messages branch, verifying correct transport selection for custom Anthropic-compatible endpoints and graceful SDK degradation.
- `tests/agent/test_auxiliary_client.py` — Comprehensive pytest suite validating the auxiliary LLM client resolution chain, credential handling, and error recovery mechanisms. It covers provider fallback logic, JWT/OAuth token validation, payment/rate-limit detection, cache eviction on transport failures, and API-specific adaptations like Kimi temperature omission and Codex reasoning translation.
- `tests/agent/test_auxiliary_config_bridge.py` — Tests the config.yaml → environment variable bridging logic shared by CLI and gateway loaders for auxiliary vision and web_extract tasks, plus vision model override behavior and DEFAULT_CONFIG shape validation.
- `tests/agent/test_auxiliary_main_first.py` — Regression tests for the "auto" → main-model-first policy, ensuring auxiliary tasks use the user's main chat model rather than cheap provider-side defaults, with explicit config overrides still winning.
- `tests/agent/test_auxiliary_named_custom_providers.py` — Tests named custom provider resolution, the "main" alias, model normalization, and api_mode propagation for auxiliary client routing, including Anthropic Messages wire protocol support.
- `tests/agent/test_auxiliary_transport_autodetect.py` — Tests transport auto-detection logic that selects the correct wire protocol (OpenAI chat.completions vs Anthropic Messages) based on endpoint URLs and explicit api_mode overrides.
- `tests/agent/test_bedrock_1m_context.py` — Tests that the 1M-context beta header is correctly emitted on AWS Bedrock Claude requests and stripped for MiniMax bearer-auth endpoints.
- `tests/agent/test_bedrock_adapter.py` — Comprehensive unit tests for the AWS Bedrock Converse API adapter, covering credential resolution, message/tool conversion, response normalization, streaming callbacks, model discovery, error classification, and stale-connection client eviction.
- `tests/agent/test_bedrock_integration.py` — Integration tests verifying Bedrock's registration in the provider registry, model catalog, runtime resolution pipeline, and auxiliary client wiring, including regression guards for model ID dot preservation and packaging extras.
- `tests/agent/test_codex_cloudflare_headers.py` — Regression tests ensuring Codex Cloudflare 403 mitigation headers (originator, User-Agent, ChatGPT-Account-ID) are consistently emitted across primary and auxiliary client initialization paths.
- `tests/agent/test_compress_focus.py` — Unit tests verifying that the `focus_topic` parameter correctly flows through the context compressor's summary generation pipeline, injecting targeted guidance into LLM prompts when provided.
- `tests/agent/test_compressor_image_tokens.py` — Tests validating image-token accounting in the context compressor, ensuring fixed token estimates per image part prevent tail-cut/prune decisions from ignoring multimodal payload costs.
- `tests/agent/test_context_compressor_summary_continuity.py` — Regression tests ensuring iterative context compression correctly handles summary continuity, preventing duplicate serialization of previous summaries and properly rehydrating handoff messages after process restarts.
- `tests/agent/test_context_compressor.py` — Comprehensive test suite for ContextCompressor covering compression thresholds, summary generation, error fallback chains, role-collision resolution, token-budget tail protection, and JSON argument truncation safety.
- `tests/agent/test_context_engine.py` — Tests for the ContextEngine abstract base class and its plugin registration slot, verifying contract enforcement, default behaviors, and PluginManager integration.
- `tests/agent/test_context_references.py` — Tests for context reference parsing and expansion, covering @file, @folder, @diff, @git, and @url references with path safety, budget gating, and async URL fetching.
- `tests/agent/test_copilot_acp_client.py` — Safety-layer regression tests for CopilotACPClient, covering permission denial, file read/write blocking, sensitive content redaction, and HOME environment propagation.
- `tests/agent/test_credential_pool_routing.py` — Tests verifying credential pool preservation through turn config resolution and the agent loop's 429/402 recovery cycle. Covers CLI and Gateway routing paths, eager fallback gating, and the full retry-same → rotate → exhaust → fallback flow.
- `tests/agent/test_credential_pool.py` — Comprehensive tests for multi-credential runtime pooling, covering selection strategies, exhaustion TTLs, env/file seeding, custom endpoint pools, lease management, and cross-process OAuth sync for Nous and OpenAI Codex providers.
- `tests/agent/test_crossloop_client_cache.py` — Tests for cross-loop async client cache isolation (fix #2681). Verifies that `_get_cached_client()` returns distinct AsyncOpenAI instances per event loop to prevent httpx deadlocks in gateway mode.
- `tests/agent/test_curator_activity.py` — Regression test ensuring curator's stale/archive transitions respect recent view activity. A skill viewed within the stale threshold must not be marked stale regardless of age.
- `tests/agent/test_curator_backup.py` — Tests for curator skill tree snapshot and rollback, including tarball creation, exclusion rules, pruning, rollback safety, and cron job skill-link restoration during rollback.
- `tests/agent/test_curator_classification.py` — Unit tests validating the curator module's classification logic for skills and jobs, ensuring correct categorization into states like active, stale, or archived based on usage metrics and configuration thresholds.
- `tests/agent/test_error_classifier.py` — Unit tests for the agent error classifier module, verifying how the system categorizes provider-specific failures, runtime exceptions, and API errors to drive retry logic and fallback routing.
- `tests/agent/test_gemini_native_adapter.py` — Validates the native Google AI Studio Gemini adapter. Tests request construction, tool call replay with thought signatures, JSON schema field stripping, response translation for reasoning and tools, HTTP client configuration, error handling, async streaming wrappers, and stream event translation for stable tool call indexing.
- `tests/agent/test_gemini_schema.py` — Validates OpenAI-to-Gemini tool parameter translation. Tests recursive schema sanitization, stripping unsupported keys like `$schema` and `additionalProperties`, dropping non-string enums to prevent HTTP 400 errors, preserving string enums, and handling nested properties and array items.
- `tests/agent/test_i18n.py` — Validates the internationalization catalog and language resolution. Tests locale file existence, key and placeholder parity across translations, language normalization and aliases, environment variable overrides, fallback behavior for missing keys or unsupported languages, and formatting semantics.
- `tests/agent/test_image_gen_registry.py` — Validates the image generation provider registry. Tests provider registration, type and name validation, overwriting behavior, sorted listing, and active provider resolution logic including single-provider auto-resolution, FAL preference, explicit configuration overrides, and fallback handling.
- `tests/agent/test_image_routing.py` — Validates per-turn image input mode decision and content building. Tests mode coercion, auxiliary vision override detection, native vs text mode selection based on model capabilities, MIME sniffing over file extensions, path hint injection, missing file skipping, and large image passthrough.
- `tests/agent/test_insights.py` — Validates the session analytics and reporting engine. Tests cost estimation, duration formatting, bar chart generation, empty and populated database handling, model/platform/tool/skill breakdowns, activity pattern tracking, source and time filtering, terminal vs gateway formatting differences, and edge cases like missing tokens or custom models.
- `tests/agent/test_kimi_coding_anthropic_thinking.py` — Validates Anthropic adapter behavior for Kimi/Moonshot endpoints. Tests suppression of `thinking` and `output_config` parameters to prevent HTTP 400 validation errors, preservation of thinking for other providers, and correct handling of unsigned `reasoning_content` during message history replay.
- `tests/agent/test_local_stream_timeout.py` — Validates local LLM provider stream timeout auto-detection. Tests `is_local_endpoint` against localhost, private IPs, container DNS, and Tailscale CGNAT ranges. Verifies automatic timeout bumping for local endpoints while respecting explicit user overrides and keeping defaults for remote hosts.
- `tests/agent/test_markdown_tables.py` — Validates markdown table realignment and formatting. Tests row splitting, divider detection, CJK and wide-character alignment, emoji handling, ASCII passthrough, vertical fallback for tables exceeding terminal width, ragged row padding, and multi-table processing within a single text block.
- `tests/agent/test_memory_provider.py` — Comprehensive test suite for the memory provider interface, manager lifecycle, plugin discovery, and tool routing.
- `tests/agent/test_memory_session_switch.py` — Tests for the `on_session_switch` hook and session ID propagation across memory providers.
- `tests/agent/test_memory_user_id.py` — Tests for per-user memory scoping via `user_id` threading from gateway to providers.
- `tests/agent/test_minimax_auxiliary_url.py` — Tests for MiniMax auxiliary client URL normalization from Anthropic-compat to OpenAI SDK paths.
- `tests/agent/test_minimax_provider.py` — Tests for MiniMax provider hardening, covering context lengths, thinking support, beta headers, and credential guards.
- `tests/agent/test_model_metadata_local_ctx.py` — Tests for local server context length querying and fallback logic across Ollama, vLLM, and LM Studio.
- `tests/agent/test_model_metadata_ssl.py` — Tests for SSL CA bundle environment variable precedence in metadata fetching.
- `tests/agent/test_model_metadata.py` — Comprehensive test suite for agent/model_metadata.py covering token estimation heuristics, context-length resolution across multiple providers (Codex OAuth, Nous Portal, Bedrock), persistent YAML caching, API metadata fetching with TTL, probe-tier fallbacks, and error-message parsing for context-limit extraction.
- `tests/agent/test_models_dev.py` — Tests for agent/models_dev.py covering the models.dev registry integration: provider-to-registry mapping, context extraction, in-memory and disk caching with TTL, and model capability detection (vision via modalities.input vs legacy attachment flag).
- `tests/agent/test_moonshot_schema.py` — Tests for agent/moonshot_schema.py covering JSON Schema sanitization required by Moonshot/Kimi's strict parameter validator: missing type inference, anyOf null-branch collapsing, enum cleanup, and tool-list traversal.
- `tests/agent/test_nous_rate_guard.py` — Tests for agent/nous_rate_guard.py covering cross-session Nous Portal rate-limit state management: recording from headers, checking remaining time, distinguishing genuine account-level 429s from upstream-capacity 429s, and auxiliary client integration.
- `tests/agent/test_onboarding.py` — Tests for agent/onboarding.py covering contextual first-touch hint helpers: flag tracking in config.yaml, busy-input and tool-progress hint generation for CLI/gateway, and OpenClaw residue detection with migration guidance.
- `tests/agent/test_openrouter_response_cache.py` — Validates the agent's caching layer for OpenRouter API responses, ensuring correct storage, retrieval, TTL expiration, and cache-busting behavior across different model configurations and retry scenarios.
- `tests/agent/test_plugin_llm.py` — Comprehensive unit tests for the plugin LLM facade, covering trust gates, message construction, JSON parsing, and async/sync invocation contracts.
- `tests/agent/test_redact.py` — Unit tests for the `agent.redact` module, verifying that sensitive data and secrets are properly masked in logs and agent-generated outputs.
- `tests/agent/test_shell_hooks_consent.py` — Tests the user consent and allowlist management flow for shell hooks, covering TTY prompts, CLI/env/config overrides, and approval persistence.
- `tests/agent/test_shell_hooks.py` — Tests the subprocess bridge and JSON translation layer for `agent.shell_hooks`, focusing on payload serialization, response parsing, and tool matcher logic.
- `tests/agent/test_subagent_stop_hook.py` — Tests the subagent_stop plugin hook fired by tools.delegate_tool.delegate_task, verifying it fires once per child in single-task and batch modes, runs on the parent thread, carries child_role metadata, and strips internal _child_role keys from serialized results.
- `tests/agent/test_subdirectory_hints.py` — Tests SubdirectoryHintTracker's progressive discovery of AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, and .cursorrules files in subdirectories, triggered by tool calls. Covers ancestor walking, deduplication, terminal command path extraction, and PermissionError resilience.
- `tests/agent/test_think_scrubber.py` — Tests StreamingThinkScrubber's contract for separating visible content from reasoning blocks in streaming deltas. Covers closed pairs, unterminated opens, orphan closes, partial tags across deltas, and the MiniMax split-open regression scenario.
- `tests/agent/test_title_generator.py` — Tests agent.title_generator's auto-generated session titles, covering LLM invocation, quote/prefix stripping, truncation, failure callbacks, background threading via maybe_auto_title, and session database integration.
- `tests/agent/test_tool_guardrails.py` — Tests ToolCallGuardrailController's detection and blocking of repeated tool failures, idempotent no-progress loops, and exact signature matching. Covers soft warnings, hard stops, and turn-based state resets.
- `tests/agent/test_tool_result_classification.py` — Tests file_mutation_result_landed helper that determines whether write_file/patch operations succeeded despite nested lint or LSP diagnostic errors, distinguishing them from top-level operation failures.
- `tests/agent/test_unsupported_parameter_retry.py` — Tests the generalized unsupported-parameter detector in agent.auxiliary_client, covering _is_unsupported_parameter_error matching across providers, back-compat temperature wrapper, and max_tokens retry gating when max_tokens is None.
- `tests/agent/test_unsupported_temperature_retry.py` — Tests the universal unsupported-temperature retry in agent.auxiliary_client, verifying reactive retry-once-without-temperature behavior for sync and async paths across multiple provider error phrasings.
- `tests/agent/test_usage_pricing.py` — Tests usage normalization across Anthropic and OpenAI-compatible providers, cache token bucket handling, pricing entry resolution from metadata, and cost estimation including subscription routes and custom endpoints.
- `tests/agent/test_video_gen_registry.py` — Tests video_gen_registry's provider registration, active provider resolution, config-based selection, and fallback behavior. Unlike image_gen, video_gen has no legacy default provider.
- `tests/agent/test_vision_resolved_args.py` — Tests that call_llm's vision path passes resolved provider/model/key/url from config to resolve_vision_provider_client, not raw arguments. Also verifies explicit base_url override preserves resolved provider.
- `tests/agent/transports/test_bedrock_transport.py` — Tests BedrockTransport's kwargs building, tool conversion, response validation, finish reason mapping, and response normalization including reasoning content extraction and already-normalized input handling.
- `tests/agent/transports/test_chat_completions.py` — Unit tests for the ChatCompletionsTransport adapter, verifying request construction, response parsing, and error handling across various provider configurations.
- `tests/agent/transports/test_codex_app_server_runtime.py` — Unit tests for the codex_app_server runtime gate, validating opt-in routing, version parsing, binary checks, and environment isolation for JSON-RPC transport.
- `tests/cli/test_branch_command.py` — Unit tests validating the `/branch` (alias `/fork`) CLI command, specifically verifying session state duplication, isolation guarantees, and lifecycle hook execution during branching operations.
- `tests/cli/test_busy_input_mode_command.py` — Tests for the /busy CLI command, covering mode switching (queue, interrupt, steer), configuration persistence, status reporting, and command registry validation.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_approval_ui.py` — Comprehensive tests for the CLI's approval and sudo prompt UI, focusing on thread-local callback registration, buffer restoration, terminal-aware truncation, and background task integration.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_background_tui_refresh.py` — Tests ensuring the TUI refreshes correctly before printing background task output, preventing UI overlap and verifying thread-safe task tracking.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_bracketed_paste_sanitizer.py` — Tests for the CLI's defensive bracketed-paste wrapper stripping, ensuring escape sequences and degraded fragments are removed while preserving legitimate text.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_browser_connect.py` — Tests for CLI browser CDP auto-launch helpers, verifying Chrome debug command construction, required flags, and platform-specific subprocess handling.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_markdown_rendering.py` — Tests the CLI's markdown rendering pipeline, verifying how markdown content is processed and displayed within the terminal interface using Rich console utilities.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_mcp_config_watch.py` — Validates the CLI's automatic MCP server reload mechanism triggered by `config.yaml` modifications.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_new_session.py` — Regression tests for CLI session lifecycle commands (`/new`, `/reset`, `/clear`), ensuring state resets and database updates.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_prefix_matching.py` — Tests the CLI's slash command prefix matching logic, ensuring correct dispatch, ambiguity resolution, and recursion safety.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_preloaded_skills.py` — Validates CLI initialization with preloaded skills, ensuring prompt injection and startup banner behavior.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_provider_resolution.py` — Tests CLI provider resolution timing and module isolation to prevent eager credential checks during initialization.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_tools_command.py` — Unit tests for the Hermes CLI `/tools` slash command, verifying tool listing, toggling, and state persistence across sessions.
- `tests/cli/test_cli_user_message_preview.py` — Tests for the submitted user message preview formatter, validating line truncation and configuration fallbacks.
- `tests/cli/test_compress_focus.py` — Tests for the `/compress <focus>` command, ensuring focus topics are extracted and passed to the compression engine.
- `tests/cli/test_cprint_bg_thread.py` — Tests for `_cprint`'s background thread cooperation with `prompt_toolkit`, verifying cross-thread scheduling and output history.
- `tests/cli/test_ctrl_enter_newline.py` — Regression tests for Ctrl+Enter newline handling across SSH, WSL, and Windows Terminal environments.
- `tests/cli/test_cwd_env_respect.py` — Tests for CLI/TUI CWD resolution in `load_cli_config()`, covering local, non-local, and gateway environments.
- `tests/cli/test_destructive_slash_confirm.py` — Tests for the destructive slash command confirmation helper, validating config gating, user choices, and modal state.
- `tests/cli/test_resume_display.py` — Tests for _display_resumed_history() and _preload_resumed_session(), verifying that resumed sessions show a compact conversation recap with correct formatting, truncation, reasoning tag stripping, and config-driven suppression.
- `tests/cli/test_save_conversation_location.py` — Regression tests for /save slash command, verifying conversation snapshots write to ~/.hermes/sessions/saved/ instead of CWD, with proper JSON payload and user-facing path hints.
- `tests/cli/test_session_boundary_hooks.py` — Tests for session boundary plugin hooks (on_session_finalize, on_session_reset), verifying they fire on /new, /reset, and CLI exit cleanup, with error isolation.
- `tests/cli/test_stream_delta_think_tag.py` — Tests for _stream_delta's handling of tags, distinguishing prose mentions from real reasoning blocks, and verifying flush recovery from false positives.
- `tests/cli/test_surrogate_sanitization.py` — Tests for surrogate character sanitization in user input and API messages, preventing json.dumps() crashes from invalid UTF-8 surrogates in reasoning output.
- `tests/cli/test_tool_progress_scrollback.py` — Tests for stacked tool progress scrollback lines in CLI TUI, verifying mode-specific behavior (all/new/off/verbose) and concurrent tool handling.
- `tests/cli/test_worktree_security.py` — Security-focused integration tests for CLI worktree setup, verifying .worktreeinclude rejects path traversal, external symlinks, and directory escapes.
- `tests/cli/test_worktree.py` — Comprehensive tests for git worktree isolation, covering creation, cleanup, .worktreeinclude handling, .gitignore management, stale pruning, and TERMINAL_CWD integration.
- `tests/conftest.py` — Central pytest configuration enforcing hermetic test isolation by stripping credential/behavioral env vars, redirecting HERMES_HOME, resetting module-level state, and guarding against accidental live-system mutations.
- `tests/cron/test_codex_execution_paths.py` — Validates that both the cron scheduler and gateway runner correctly handle OpenAI Codex 401 authentication errors by triggering credential refresh and resuming execution.
- `tests/cron/test_compute_next_run_last_run_at.py` — Regression tests ensuring compute_next_run anchors cron schedules to last_run_at rather than the current time, preventing schedule drift after gateway restarts.
- `tests/cron/test_cron_context_from.py` — Tests the context_from feature that chains cron jobs by injecting the latest output of referenced jobs into a downstream job's prompt.
- `tests/cron/test_cron_inactivity_timeout.py` — Validates the cron scheduler's inactivity-based timeout mechanism, ensuring idle agents are interrupted while active ones complete normally.
- `tests/cron/test_cron_no_agent.py` — Tests the no_agent cron job mode, which executes shell scripts directly without invoking the LLM, including validation, execution, and silent output handling.
- `tests/cron/test_cron_prompt_injection_skill.py` — Regression tests ensuring that skill content dynamically loaded into cron job prompts is scanned for injection payloads before execution.
- `tests/cron/test_cron_script.py` — Comprehensive tests for cron job script execution, covering path resolution, output injection into prompts, timeout handling, and strict path containment security.
- `tests/cron/test_cron_workdir.py` — Tests per-job workdir support in the cron scheduler, covering path normalization, job CRUD plumbing, tool JSON round-trips, sequential execution partitioning, and TERMINAL_CWD mutation/restoration during job runs.
- `tests/cron/test_file_permissions.py` — Tests file permission hardening for cron storage directories, job files, output files, and global Hermes config files, verifying 0700 for directories and 0600 for sensitive files.
- `tests/cron/test_jobs.py` — Comprehensive tests for cron job lifecycle: schedule parsing (duration, interval, cron, ISO), next-run computation, CRUD operations, pause/resume, run tracking, crash-safety advancement, due-job detection with grace windows, and concurrent write safety.
- `tests/cron/test_rewrite_skill_refs.py` — Tests the curator integration `rewrite_skill_refs` that updates cron job skill references after skill consolidation or pruning, ensuring jobs continue to load the correct skills at runtime.
- `tests/cron/test_scheduler_mcp_init.py` — Regression test ensuring MCP server discovery is skipped for `no_agent=True` cron jobs, avoiding wasted initialization cost for script-only executions.
- `tests/cron/test_scheduler.py` — Test suite for the cron scheduler module, focusing on origin resolution and job dispatch mechanics.
- `tests/e2e/matrix_xsign_bootstrap/test_bootstrap.py` — End-to-end test for Matrix cross-signing auto-bootstrap using a Dockerized Continuwuity homeserver. Verifies unpadded base64 keyid publication, idempotent bootstrap on restart, and device signing by the new SSK.
- `tests/e2e/test_discord_adapter.py` — E2E tests for Discord adapter's mention stripping and slash command detection, including edge cases around auto-threading and nickname mentions.
- `tests/e2e/test_platform_commands.py` — E2E tests for gateway-level slash commands across Telegram and Discord, verifying dispatch, session lifecycle, authorization, and send failure resilience.
- `tests/environments/benchmarks/test_terminalbench2_env_security.py` — Security tests for Terminal-Bench 2 archive extraction, verifying protection against path traversal and symlink attacks during base64 tar unpacking.
- `tests/fakes/fake_ha_server.py` — In-process fake Home Assistant server for integration testing, mimicking REST and WebSocket APIs used by the Hermes agent.
- `tests/gateway/_plugin_adapter_loader.py` — Shared helper to safely load platform plugin `adapter.py` modules in tests without polluting `sys.path` or causing `sys.modules` collisions.
- `tests/gateway/conftest.py` — Shared pytest fixtures and collection-time guards for gateway tests, including comprehensive Telegram/Discord mocks and anti-pattern detection for plugin adapter imports.
- `tests/gateway/feishu_helpers.py` — Shared factory functions for constructing Feishu adapter test fixtures, including sender objects, messages, adapter skeletons, and deduplication state.
- `tests/gateway/restart_test_helpers.py` — Test utilities for simulating gateway restart scenarios, providing a mock adapter and a pre-wired `GatewayRunner` instance.
- `tests/gateway/test_7100_transient_failure_transcript.py` — Unit tests ensuring transient provider failures (429, timeouts, 500s) preserve user messages in the transcript, while context-overflow failures correctly skip them.
- `tests/gateway/test_agent_cache.py` — Integration tests validating the gateway's AIAgent caching mechanism, ensuring agents are correctly reused across messages when configurations match and properly isolated when they differ.
- `tests/gateway/test_api_server_multimodal.py` — Validates the gateway API server's handling of inline image inputs across /v1/chat/completions and /v1/responses endpoints, ensuring the multimodal normalization pipeline correctly processes and routes vision data.
- `tests/gateway/test_api_server_normalize.py` — Tests the `_normalize_chat_content` utility that converts array-based message parts into plain text for logging and chat history.
- `tests/gateway/test_api_server_runs.py` — End-to-end tests for the `/v1/runs` API endpoints, covering run lifecycle management, SSE event streaming, and agent interruption.
- `tests/gateway/test_bluebubbles.py` — Test suite for the BlueBubbles iMessage gateway adapter, verifying platform configuration parsing and adapter instantiation helpers.
- `tests/gateway/test_busy_session_ack.py` — Unit tests for the gateway runner's busy-session acknowledgment logic, ensuring users receive immediate feedback when messaging during active agent runs.
- `tests/gateway/test_compress_command.py` — Unit tests validating the gateway's /compress command behavior, specifically focusing on user-facing messaging, callback invocations, and interaction with the context compression lifecycle.
- `tests/gateway/test_compress_focus.py` — Tests focus topic propagation for the /compress <focus> gateway command.
- `tests/gateway/test_compress_plugin_engine.py` — Regression tests ensuring /compress remains compatible with third-party context engine plugins.
- `tests/gateway/test_config_cwd_bridge.py` — Tests the config.yaml to environment variable bridge for terminal working directory and backend settings.
- `tests/gateway/test_config_env_bridge_authority.py` — Regression tests enforcing config.yaml authority over stale .env values during gateway startup.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_allowed_channels.py` — Regression test verifying that wildcard "*" entries in Discord channel configuration lists correctly expand to permit all channels rather than being treated as literal strings.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_allowed_mentions.py` — Tests the `_build_allowed_mentions` helper to verify safe defaults block @everyone/@here pings while allowing user/reply pings, with env var overrides.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_attachment_download.py` — Tests Discord attachment caching paths, prioritizing authenticated `att.read()` over URL downloads to avoid CDN 403s and SSRF false positives.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_bot_auth_bypass.py` — Regression tests for `DISCORD_ALLOW_BOTS` and `DISCORD_ALLOWED_ROLES` ensuring bot messages bypass human allowlists when explicitly permitted.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_bot_filter.py` — Tests Discord bot message filtering logic based on the `DISCORD_ALLOW_BOTS` configuration setting.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_free_response.py` — Validates Discord adapter behavior regarding free-response defaults and mention gating mechanisms.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_imports.py` — Ensures the Discord gateway adapter gracefully degrades when the `discord.py` dependency is missing, preventing import-time crashes.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_media_metadata.py` — Verifies that Discord adapter's media sending methods accept a `metadata` keyword argument to align with upstream contracts.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_model_picker.py` — Regression tests for the Discord `/model` picker UI, verifying interaction flow, message editing, and callback execution without `sys.modules` pollution.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_opus.py` — Source-inspection tests ensuring Discord Opus codec loading prioritizes `ctypes.util.find_library`, guards Homebrew fallbacks, and logs decode errors.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_race_polish.py` — Validates that concurrent `join_voice_channel` calls serialize through a per-guild lock, preventing duplicate `channel.connect()` invocations.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_reactions.py` — Tests Discord message reactions tied to processing lifecycle hooks, ensuring reaction updates align with message handling outcomes.
- `tests/gateway/test_discord_slash_commands.py` — Test suite for native Discord slash command fast-paths, specifically validating thread creation and auto-threading behaviors within the gateway integration.
- `tests/gateway/test_email.py` — Comprehensive test suite for the Email gateway adapter, validating environment-driven configuration, IMAP/SMTP lifecycle, message parsing, attachment caching, reply threading, sender allowlisting, and NetEase-specific IMAP ID extension support.
- `tests/gateway/test_ephemeral_reply.py` — Tests the `EphemeralReply` auto-deletion mechanism in gateway adapters, verifying TTL unwrapping, platform capability detection, background task scheduling, and graceful degradation for adapters lacking native delete support.
- `tests/gateway/test_extract_local_files.py` — Validates the `extract_local_files()` utility for auto-detecting bare local media paths in model outputs, covering regex matching, code-block exclusion, URL rejection, tilde expansion, deduplication, and text cleanup.
- `tests/gateway/test_fallback_eviction.py` — Regression tests for gateway agent cache eviction logic, ensuring failed runs do not trigger cached agent eviction to prevent CPU-intensive MCP reinitialization loops while allowing eviction on successful fallback activations.
- `tests/gateway/test_fast_command.py` — Tests the `/fast` slash command and Priority Processing routing, validating `service_tier` config persistence, runtime agent kwargs injection, model support gating, and end-to-end flow from command handling to agent initialization.
- `tests/gateway/test_feishu_approval_buttons.py` — Tests Feishu adapter's interactive card system for command approvals and update prompts, covering card payload generation, state persistence, thread-safe callback routing, inline card updates, and response file synchronization.
- `tests/gateway/test_feishu_bot_admission.py` — Unit tests validating the Feishu platform adapter's bot-sender admission logic, ensuring messages from bot accounts are correctly filtered or permitted based on gateway authorization policies.
- `tests/gateway/test_feishu_bot_auth_bypass.py` — Regression tests ensuring Feishu bot senders admitted by the adapter bypass gateway-level user allowlists. Validates that `FEISHU_ALLOW_BOTS` policies correctly authorize bots in `GatewayRunner._is_user_authorized` without leaking to other platforms.
- `tests/gateway/test_feishu_comment_rules.py` — Unit tests for the Feishu comment rule engine, validating a 3-tier access control system for document comments. Covers rule parsing, exact/wildcard/top-level fallback resolution, and field-by-field inheritance.
- `tests/gateway/test_homeassistant.py` — Tests for the Home Assistant gateway adapter, covering state change formatting, event filtering, per-entity cooldowns, config parsing, and REST-based message sending.
- `tests/gateway/test_hooks.py` — Tests for the gateway event hook system, covering registry initialization, filesystem discovery, sync/async handler execution, wildcard matching, and decision-style value collection.
- `tests/gateway/test_insights_unicode_flags.py` — Tests for Unicode dash normalization in the `/insights` command, ensuring iOS Telegram's auto-converted em/en dashes are mapped back to standard `--` flags.
- `tests/gateway/test_internal_event_bypass_pairing.py` — Regression tests ensuring synthetic internal events (e.g., background process completion) bypass user authorization and DM pairing flows.
- `tests/gateway/test_interrupt_key_match.py` — Tests verifying interrupt key consistency between platform adapters and the gateway, ensuring session keys (not raw chat IDs) are used for interrupt detection.
- `tests/gateway/test_irc_adapter.py` — Tests for the IRC platform adapter, covering protocol parsing, config resolution, message dispatch, markdown stripping, CRLF injection prevention, and standalone cron delivery.
- `tests/gateway/test_kanban_notifier.py` — Tests for the kanban notification watcher, covering subscription claim/rewind logic, adapter disconnect handling, send failure recovery, and terminal event redelivery.
- `tests/gateway/test_keep_typing_timeout.py` — Tests for `BasePlatformAdapter._keep_typing` per-tick timeout bounding, preventing slow HTTP round-trips from stalling the typing indicator refresh loop.
- `tests/gateway/test_line_plugin.py` — Comprehensive test suite for the LINE platform adapter, validating webhook signature verification, chat source resolution, three-tier allowlist gating, event deduplication, and the RequestCache state machine.
- `tests/gateway/test_matrix_exec_approval.py` — Tests for the Matrix adapter's execution approval workflow, verifying reaction-based prompt registration and pending approval resolution via emoji reactions.
- `tests/gateway/test_matrix_mention.py` — Extensive tests for Matrix mention detection, stripping, outbound mention formatting, require-mention gating, auto-threading, and YAML-to-env config bridging.
- `tests/gateway/test_matrix_voice.py` — Tests for Matrix voice message support (MSC3245), validating voice detection, local caching fallbacks, and native voice message sending.
- `tests/gateway/test_matrix.py` — Comprehensive pytest suite for the Matrix platform adapter, validating configuration parsing, E2EE bootstrap, message routing, media handling, proxy setup, and HTML sanitization against XSS vectors.
- `tests/gateway/test_mattermost.py` — Test suite for the Mattermost platform adapter, verifying media handling, retry logic, and gateway integration using mocked async components.
- `tests/gateway/test_media_download_retry.py` — Test suite validating media download retry logic across base, Slack, and Mattermost adapters, focusing on HTTP error handling and caching behavior.
- `tests/gateway/test_native_image_buffer_isolation.py` — Validates that GatewayRunner isolates native image buffers per session, preventing cross-session image leakage and ensuring non-image messages don't clear pending image paths.
- `tests/gateway/test_notice_delivery.py` — Tests `_deliver_platform_notice` routing logic, verifying fallback to public delivery when private delivery fails and default public routing when private mode isn't configured.
- `tests/gateway/test_pairing.py` — Comprehensive test suite for the DM pairing security system, covering code generation, rate limiting, approval flows, lockouts, expiry, and secure disk persistence.
- `tests/gateway/test_pending_drain_no_recursion.py` — Regression tests for #17758 ensuring chained pending-message drains spawn fresh tasks instead of recursing, keeping call stacks bounded and session guards intact.
- `tests/gateway/test_pending_drain_race.py` — Regression tests for pending-drain and finally-cleanup races, ensuring `_active_sessions` stays live to prevent duplicate agents and late-arrival messages aren't dropped.
- `tests/gateway/test_pending_event_none.py` — Tests pending follow-up extraction logic in recursive `_run_agent` calls, ensuring `channel_prompt` handles `None` safely and control interrupts aren't recycled as user input.
- `tests/gateway/test_pii_redaction.py` — Tests PII redaction in gateway session context prompts, verifying deterministic hashing of user/chat IDs while preserving platform-specific mention requirements.
- `tests/gateway/test_platform_base.py` — Extensive test suite for `gateway/platforms/base.py`, covering message parsing, media/image extraction, truncation, UTF-16 length handling, and proxy configuration.
- `tests/gateway/test_platform_connected_checkers.py` — Verifies every gateway platform has a connection checker, ensuring `GatewayConfig.get_connected_platforms()` doesn't silently drop platforms with bespoke auth requirements.
- `tests/gateway/test_platform_http_client_limits.py` — Tests the shared `httpx.Limits` helper for long-lived platform adapters, verifying keepalive tuning, env overrides, and structural guards against socket leaks.
- `tests/gateway/test_platform_reconnect.py` — Tests the gateway platform reconnection watcher, covering startup isolation, failure queuing, retryable vs non-retryable error handling, and runtime disconnect management.
- `tests/gateway/test_platform_registry.py` — Tests the platform adapter registry, dynamic Platform enum behavior, GatewayConfig integration, and the apply_yaml_config_fn hook dispatch pipeline for plugin-registered platforms.
- `tests/gateway/test_plugin_platform_interface.py` — Interface compliance tests that dynamically discover all bundled platform plugins and verify each implements the required registration contract and adapter interface.
- `tests/gateway/test_post_delivery_callback_chaining.py` — Tests the post-delivery callback chaining mechanism on BasePlatformAdapter, verifying ordered execution, exception isolation, and generation-based stale rejection.
- `tests/gateway/test_pre_gateway_dispatch.py` — Tests the pre_gateway_dispatch plugin hook that intercepts incoming messages before auth and agent dispatch, supporting skip, rewrite, and allow actions.
- `tests/gateway/test_proxy_mode.py` — Tests gateway proxy mode that forwards agent requests to a remote API server via HTTP/SSE, including URL resolution, request building, streaming parsing, and error handling.
- `tests/gateway/test_qqbot.py` — Test suite for the QQ Bot platform adapter, validating webhook handling, async lifecycle management, and configuration routing within the Hermes gateway.
- `tests/gateway/test_replay_entry_fields.py` — Tests the `_build_replay_entry` function in `gateway.run`, verifying how the gateway reconstructs `agent_history` from persisted transcripts and handles reasoning/thinking fields during session replay.
- `tests/gateway/test_reply_to_injection.py` — Tests the `_prepare_inbound_message_text` logic that injects `[Replying to: "..."]` disambiguation pointers into inbound messages. Ensures pointers survive history overlaps and respect length limits.
- `tests/gateway/test_restart_drain.py` — Covers gateway restart and drain mechanics, including `/restart` command handling, queue/interrupt modes, timeout configuration, detached subprocess spawning, and shutdown notification routing to active sessions.
- `tests/gateway/test_restart_notification.py` — Tests the persistence and delivery of restart notifications via `.restart_notify.json`. Validates marker creation, systemd/detached path selection, and routing metadata serialization for post-restart user pings.
- `tests/gateway/test_retry_replacement.py` — Regression tests validating the /retry command's replacement semantics within the gateway, ensuring correct content substitution and state preservation during message or tool call retries.
- `tests/gateway/test_retry_response.py` — Regression tests ensuring /retry returns the agent's actual response instead of None, and gracefully handles sessions with no prior user messages.
- `tests/gateway/test_run_cleanup_progress.py` — Tests the opt-in cleanup of temporary tool-progress bubbles after successful agent runs, ensuring failed runs preserve breadcrumbs and unsupported adapters skip deletion.
- `tests/gateway/test_run_progress_interrupt.py` — Verifies that tool-progress events are suppressed when an agent is interrupted, preventing stale progress bubbles from appearing after a stop command.
- `tests/gateway/test_run_progress_topics.py` — Tests topic-aware progress updates in the gateway, ensuring platform adapters correctly send and edit progress messages based on tool execution topics.
- `tests/gateway/test_send_multiple_images.py` — Tests the `send_multiple_images` functionality, verifying native batching behavior across different platform adapters and fallback mechanisms for platforms lacking native support.
- `tests/gateway/test_send_retry.py` — Unit tests for BasePlatformAdapter._send_with_retry and error classification helpers, focusing on transient vs. permanent failure handling.
- `tests/gateway/test_session_model_override_routing.py` — Regression tests ensuring session-scoped model and provider overrides take precedence over global runtime configuration during agent initialization and background task execution.
- `tests/gateway/test_session_model_reset.py` — Tests verifying that `/new` and `/reset` commands correctly clear session-scoped model, reasoning, and pending note overrides without affecting other sessions.
- `tests/gateway/test_session_race_guard.py` — Tests for the sentinel-based race guard that prevents duplicate agent spawns and manages message queuing during concurrent session requests.
- `tests/gateway/test_session_reset_notify.py` — Tests for session auto-reset policies, verifying reason tracking, activity detection, and notification routing across platforms.
- `tests/gateway/test_session_split_brain_11016.py` — Regression tests for issue #11016, covering adapter-side task cancellation, stale lock self-healing, and runner-side generation guards.
- `tests/gateway/test_session_state_cleanup.py` — Tests for `_release_running_agent_state` and SessionDB shutdown, ensuring synchronized cleanup of running-agent dicts and WAL lock release.
- `tests/gateway/test_session_store_prune.py` — Tests for `SessionStore.prune_old_entries`, verifying age-based eviction, active process preservation, thread safety, and disk persistence.
- `tests/gateway/test_session.py` — Pytest suite validating gateway session management logic, likely covering session creation, persistence, and state transitions using mocked dependencies.
- `tests/gateway/test_signal.py` — Comprehensive test suite for the Signal messenger platform adapter, covering configuration loading, adapter initialization, attachment fetching, media sending, rate-limit handling, typing indicators, quote extraction, and content filtering.
- `tests/gateway/test_slack_approval_buttons.py` — Tests for Slack Block Kit approval buttons, thread context fetching, and session key resolution, ensuring proper UI interaction, deduplication, and context formatting for agent command approvals.
- `tests/gateway/test_slack_channel_skills.py` — Tests for Slack channel-to-skill binding resolution, verifying that auto-skills are correctly mapped to DMs, threads, and message events based on configuration.
- `tests/gateway/test_slack_mention.py` — Test suite for Slack mention gating logic, validating require_mention and free_response_channels configurations. Mirrors the structure and patterns of test_whatsapp_group_gating.py.
- `tests/gateway/test_slash_access_dispatch.py` — Integration tests for slash command access control within `GatewayRunner._handle_message`. Exercises admin/user allowlists, always-allowed command floors, and DM vs. group scope isolation using a stubbed session store and direct runner instantiation.
- `tests/gateway/test_status.py` — Tests gateway runtime status tracking, PID file atomicity, stale PID cleanup, runtime lock acquisition/release, takeover/planned-stop markers, and cross-platform process cmdline reading fallbacks.
- `tests/gateway/test_steer_command.py` — Tests the /steer command handler, verifying it injects text into a running agent's next tool result without interrupting, falls back to queueing for pending agents, and handles missing steer() methods gracefully.
- `tests/gateway/test_step_callback_compat.py` — Tests backward compatibility for the gateway's step callback normalization, ensuring prev_tools (dicts or strings) are correctly converted to a tool_names string list for external hooks.
- `tests/gateway/test_sticker_cache.py` — Tests the sticker description cache, covering JSON load/save resilience, cache overwrites, and the exact formatting of static/animated sticker injection strings for the agent prompt.
- `tests/gateway/test_stream_consumer_draft.py` — Tests native draft streaming support in GatewayStreamConsumer, verifying transport selection, draft animation lifecycle, fallback on failure, and distinct draft IDs across responses and tool boundaries.
- `tests/gateway/test_stream_consumer_fresh_final.py` — Regression tests for the fresh-final delivery path, ensuring long-lived streaming previews are replaced by new messages with best-effort deletion, and verifying config defaults and adapter contract compliance.
- `tests/gateway/test_stream_consumer_thread_routing.py` — Regression tests for thread/topic routing in GatewayStreamConsumer, verifying initial_reply_to_id is correctly passed on the first send, preserved during overflow chunking, and handled by Feishu's fallback routing.
- `tests/gateway/test_stream_consumer.py` — Unit tests validating the GatewayStreamConsumer's handling of media directives during streaming operations.
- `tests/gateway/test_teams.py` — Test suite for the Microsoft Teams platform adapter plugin, verifying its integration with the Hermes gateway's message routing, authentication, and event handling pipelines.
- `tests/gateway/test_telegram_format.py` — Unit tests validating Telegram MarkdownV2 formatting utilities, ensuring standard markdown is safely escaped and converted to prevent adapter crashes or malformed messages.
- `tests/gateway/test_telegram_reply_mode.py` — Test suite validating the Telegram adapter's reply_to_mode configuration, specifically verifying how multi-chunk message threading behaves under different operational modes.
- `tests/gateway/test_telegram_reply_quote.py` — Tests inbound partial-quote extraction for Telegram replies. Ensures the adapter prioritizes user-selected quote substrings over full replied-to message text to prevent context pollution, with fallbacks for captions and empty quotes.
- `tests/gateway/test_telegram_text_batch_perf.py` — Tests performance tuning helpers for Telegram text batching. Validates the `_env_float_clamped` guard against malformed floats and verifies adaptive delay tiers compose correctly with operator-configured caps and split thresholds.
- `tests/gateway/test_telegram_text_batching.py` — Tests inbound text message aggregation for Telegram. Verifies that rapid successive messages from the same chat are buffered and merged before dispatch, while isolating different chats and cleaning up internal state post-flush.
- `tests/gateway/test_telegram_thread_fallback.py` — Tests fallback routing logic for Telegram forum topics or threads. Ensures messages reach the correct session context when primary thread identifiers are missing, invalid, or unsupported by the client.
- `tests/gateway/test_text_batching.py` — Tests text message batching across Discord, Matrix, WeCom, Telegram, and Feishu adapters. Verifies that rapid successive messages from the same chat are aggregated before dispatch, with adaptive delays for near-limit chunks and asyncio.shield protection against cancellation propagation.
- `tests/gateway/test_title_command.py` — Tests the `/title` slash command for setting and showing session titles. Covers title conflicts, control character sanitization, length limits, cross-platform operation, and `/new <title>` integration with duplicate-title warnings.
- `tests/gateway/test_transcript_offset.py` — Regression tests for a transcript history offset bug where `session_meta` and `system` messages caused message loss. Validates the fix using filtered history length for slicing, including queued follow-up recursion.
- `tests/gateway/test_tts_media_routing.py` — Tests cross-platform audio media routing for Telegram. Verifies that FLAC and non-voice OGG files route to `send_document`, while MP3 and voice-tagged OGG route to `send_voice`, both in background processing and streaming delivery paths.
- `tests/gateway/test_unauthorized_dm_behavior.py` — Tests unauthorized DM handling across platforms. Covers allowlist resolution, WhatsApp LID mapping, wildcard authorization, group-specific allowlists, pairing defaults, rate limiting, and the allowlist-derived 'ignore' default behavior.
- `tests/gateway/test_unavailable_skill_hint.py` — Tests `_check_unavailable_skill` for correct frontmatter-based slug matching. Fixes a bug where directory names were compared instead of declared skill names, causing disabled/missing skill hints to fail for multi-word skills.
- `tests/gateway/test_unknown_command.py` — Tests unknown slash command handling and the `command:<name>` hook system. Verifies that unrecognized commands return user guidance instead of leaking to the LLM, and that hooks can deny, handle, allow, or rewrite commands.
- `tests/gateway/test_update_command.py` — Tests for the `/update` gateway slash command, covering subprocess spawning, binary resolution, pending marker persistence, and legacy post-restart notification delivery.
- `tests/gateway/test_update_streaming.py` — Tests for the `--gateway` streaming update mode, covering file-based IPC prompts, live output polling, message interception, and stash restore with custom input functions.
- `tests/gateway/test_usage_command.py` — Tests for the `/usage` gateway command, verifying agent cache lookup priority, token/cost reporting, and account-limits section rendering.
- `tests/gateway/test_verbose_command.py` — Tests for the `/verbose` gateway command, verifying config-gated tool_progress mode cycling with per-platform isolation.
- `tests/gateway/test_vision_memory_leak.py` — Regression tests for `_enrich_message_with_vision`, verifying that memory-context fences leaked by auxiliary vision LLMs are scrubbed before reaching the user.
- `tests/gateway/test_voice_command.py` — Unit tests for the gateway's `/voice` slash command, auto voice reply routing, and Discord voice channel TTS handling. Validates state persistence, per-chat/platform isolation, deduplication logic to prevent double audio, and adapter synchronization.
- `tests/gateway/test_voice_mode_platform_isolation.py` — Tests voice mode state isolation across messaging platforms. Verifies platform-prefixed key generation, legacy key migration, invalid mode filtering, and adapter sync logic to prevent cross-platform chat ID collisions.
- `tests/gateway/test_weak_credential_guard.py` — Tests gateway startup validation that rejects weak or placeholder tokens for platform configs and API server keys. Ensures real tokens pass, empty tokens warn, and network-exposed servers block placeholder keys.
- `tests/gateway/test_webhook_adapter.py` — Comprehensive unit tests for the generic webhook platform adapter. Covers HMAC validation, prompt rendering, event filtering, HTTP handling, idempotency, rate limiting, body limits, and cross-platform delivery routing.
- `tests/gateway/test_webhook_deliver_only.py` — Tests the `deliver_only` webhook route mode, which pushes rendered notifications directly to target adapters without invoking the agent. Validates bypass behavior, HTTP codes, startup checks, and security invariants.
- `tests/gateway/test_webhook_dynamic_routes.py` — Tests dynamic route loading for the webhook adapter from a JSON file. Verifies merging with static routes, mtime-gated reloading, file removal handling, and graceful corruption recovery.
- `tests/gateway/test_webhook_integration.py` — Integration tests for the webhook adapter exercising end-to-end flows. Covers GitHub PR triggers, skill injection, cross-platform Telegram delivery, and GitHub comment posting via CLI.
- `tests/gateway/test_webhook_signature_rate_limit.py` — Tests that HMAC signature validation occurs before rate limiting to prevent invalid requests from consuming quota. Verifies correct ordering under mixed valid/invalid traffic.
- `tests/gateway/test_wecom_callback.py` — Tests the WeCom callback-mode adapter. Covers AES-CBC crypto roundtrips, XML event construction, corp/user scoping, send routing, fallback logic, and poll loop dispatch.
- `tests/gateway/test_wecom.py` — Unit tests for the WeCom platform adapter, validating webhook signature verification, payload parsing, and message routing into the gateway's unified event contract.
- `tests/gateway/test_ws_auth_retry.py` — Tests authentication-aware retry logic for Mattermost WebSocket and Matrix sync loops, verifying that specific auth errors trigger token refreshes while other errors follow standard backoff.
- `tests/gateway/test_yolo_command.py` — Ensures the `/yolo` slash command toggles approval-bypass mode strictly within the invoking session's scope, preventing global or cross-session state leakage.
- `tests/hermes_cli/conftest.py` — Provides shared pytest fixtures for Hermes CLI kanban tests, primarily patching profile existence checks to allow synthetic assignees to spawn tasks.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_ai_gateway_models.py` — Verifies the translation and filtering logic for Vercel AI Gateway model metadata, ensuring pricing fields map correctly and free models are prioritized.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_anthropic_model_flow_stale_oauth.py` — Tests the stale OAuth token detection logic in the `hermes model` CLI flow, ensuring users are prompted to re-authenticate when tokens expire and no valid refresh credentials exist.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_anthropic_oauth_flow.py` — Validates that the Anthropic OAuth setup flow prioritizes valid Claude Code credentials over stale environment variables, clearing outdated tokens upon successful setup.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_atomic_yaml_write.py` — Tests for the `utils.atomic_yaml_write` utility, verifying crash-safe YAML serialization, temporary file cleanup on exceptions, and optional extra content appending.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_auth_codex_provider.py` — Tests for OpenAI Codex authentication in `hermes_cli.auth`, covering token persistence, JWT expiration detection, OAuth refresh error parsing, CLI token import, and login flow branching.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_auth_commands.py` — Comprehensive tests for `hermes_cli.auth_commands` credential pool management, covering add/remove/reset/list operations, credential source suppression, env-seeded credential cleanup, and provider-specific auth flows.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_auth_nous_provider.py` — Regression test suite validating the intersection of Nous provider OAuth token refresh cycles and agent-key minting workflows.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_backup.py` — Comprehensive test suite for Hermes backup, import, and snapshot commands. Covers zip creation with exclusion rules, SQLite safe-copy, path traversal blocking, profile wrapper generation, quick state snapshots, pre-update/pre-migration auto-backups, and rotation pruning.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_banner_git_state.py` — Tests for banner version label formatting with git state. Covers upstream/local commit display, carried commit counting, and subprocess-based git state probing.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_banner_skills.py` — Tests for banner's skill display logic. Covers delegation to `_find_all_skills`, disabled skill filtering, category grouping, null-category fallback, and import failure resilience.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_banner.py` — Tests for banner toolset name normalization and welcome banner rendering. Covers legacy suffix stripping, OSC-8 hyperlinks to GitHub releases, and multi-source data aggregation.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_bedrock_model_picker.py` — Tests for AWS Bedrock integration in the model picker. Covers live model discovery, region-aware routing, provider overlay registration, and credential-gated visibility.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_chat_skills_flag.py` — Tests for CLI skills flag parsing in the chat command. Covers top-level `-s` flag defaulting to chat, subcommand flag acceptance, image flag, and combined flag interactions.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_claw.py` — Unit tests for the Hermes CLI's `claw` subcommand suite.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_commands.py` — Test suite validating the central command registry and CLI command dispatch mechanisms.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_config.py` — Test suite for hermes_cli configuration management, currently containing only a module docstring in the provided excerpt.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_custom_provider_model_switch.py` — Validates the `hermes model switch` CLI command when targeting custom providers, ensuring correct configuration updates, credential routing, and fallback behavior.
