- `tests/hermes_cli/test_dashboard_browser_safe_imports.py` — Static analysis test verifying that the Hermes dashboard frontend avoids importing the `@nous-research/ui` root barrel to prevent browser bundling issues.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_dashboard_lifecycle_flags.py` — Unit tests for the `hermes dashboard --status` and `--stop` CLI flags, verifying process detection, termination logic, and dependency isolation.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_doctor.py` — Comprehensive test suite for the `hermes doctor` CLI diagnostic command, covering platform detection, provider credential probing, .env encoding safety, tool availability gating, memory provider validation, and Vercel/GitHub environment checks.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_env_load_cache.py` — Tests for the process-level `.env` loading cache in `hermes_cli.config`, verifying that repeated reads are fast, mtime/size changes trigger re-reads, and writer functions properly invalidate the cache.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_env_loader.py` — Tests for `hermes_cli.env_loader.load_hermes_dotenv()`, verifying user `.env` takes precedence over project `.env`, both override stale shell values, and project files are sanitized before loading.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_env_sanitize_on_load.py` — Tests for `.env` sanitization during load to prevent token duplication (issue #8908), verifying that concatenated `KEY=VALUE` pairs on a single line are correctly split.
- `tests/hermes_cli/test_fallback_cmd.py` — Tests for the `hermes fallback` CLI subcommand, covering fallback provider chain management (list/add/remove/clear), legacy config migration, and argparse wiring.

- `tests/tools/test_send_message_missing_platforms.py` — Test suite validating message dispatch behavior when platform adapters like Mattermost are missing, unconfigured, or fail to initialize.
- `tests/tools/test_session_search.py` — Unit tests for the session search tool, validating query parsing, result filtering, and integration with the session store.
- `tests/tools/test_skill_usage.py` — Comprehensive test suite for tools/skill_usage.py, validating sidecar telemetry tracking, provenance filtering, state transitions, and archive/restore operations for agent-created skills while ensuring bundled and hub-installed skills are excluded from all telemetry and mutation paths.
- `tests/tools/test_skill_view_path_check.py` — Regression tests for the skill_view path boundary check, verifying that `Path.is_relative_to()` correctly handles cross-platform path validation and prevents the Windows backslash separator bug that caused false-positive path escape detections.
- `tests/tools/test_skill_view_traversal.py` — Regression tests for issue #220, validating that skill_view's file_path parameter blocks path traversal attacks including dotdot sequences, symlink escapes, and sensitive file access outside the skill directory.
- `tests/tools/test_skills_guard.py` — Extensive test suite for tools/skills_guard.py security scanner, covering trust level resolution, verdict determination, install policy enforcement, pattern-based threat detection, directory structure validation, symlink escape detection, and content hashing.
- `tests/tools/test_skills_hub_clawhub.py` — Unit tests for ClawHubSource in tools/skills_hub.py, validating search fallback chains, cache poisoning repair, exact slug resolution, inspect/fetch workflows, version resolution, and private URL blocking during skill downloads.
- `tests/tools/test_skills_hub.py` — Test suite for the skills hub module, specifically targeting source adapters responsible for fetching and validating skill definitions from external providers.
- `tests/tools/test_skills_tool.py` — Unit tests validating the skills tool's discovery, loading, and execution boundaries, ensuring safe skill resolution and proper error handling during agent interactions.
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_compound_background.py` — Regression tests for `_rewrite_compound_background`, a bash command rewriter that prevents orphaned subshell processes when agents background compound commands like `A && B &`.
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_config_env_sync.py` — Regression tests ensuring terminal config keys are bridged from `config.yaml` to `TERMINAL_*` env vars across all three startup code paths (CLI, gateway, config CLI).
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_exit_semantics.py` — Tests for `_interpret_exit_code`, which maps known command exit codes to human-readable semantic notes (e.g., grep exit 1 = no matches).
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_foreground_timeout_cap.py` — Tests for the foreground timeout cap that rejects commands with explicit timeouts exceeding `FOREGROUND_MAX_TIMEOUT` (600s), redirecting agents to use `background=true`.
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_none_command_guard.py` — Regression tests ensuring `None` commands are handled gracefully by `_transform_sudo_command` and `terminal_tool` without crashing.
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_output_transform_hook.py` — Tests for the `transform_terminal_output` plugin hook that lets plugins intercept and modify terminal command output before it reaches the agent.
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_requirements.py` — Tests for `check_terminal_requirements()`, validating backend-specific prerequisites (SSH host, Modal tokens, Vercel auth, runtime support) before terminal tool activation.
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_task_cwd.py` — Regression tests for task/session-scoped CWD propagation in `terminal_tool`, ensuring ACP-updated task CWDs are honored by foreground commands.
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_timeout_output.py` — Tests verifying that `LocalEnvironment.execute()` preserves partial stdout output when a command times out, rather than discarding it.
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_tool_pty_fallback.py` — Tests for automatic PTY disabling when background commands require piped stdin (e.g., `gh auth login --with-token`), preventing PTY allocation failures.
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_tool_requirements.py` — Tests for terminal and code execution tool availability resolution across different backend configurations (local, managed Modal, Vercel sandbox).
- `tests/tools/test_terminal_tool.py` — Regression tests for sudo command detection, password handling, caching, and workdir validation in `terminal_tool`.
- `tests/tools/test_threaded_process_handle.py` — Tests for `_ThreadedProcessHandle`, a process adapter that wraps SDK backend execution functions with a subprocess-like interface.
- `tests/tools/test_tirith_security.py` — Test suite for the Tirith security scanning subsystem, validating vulnerability detection, policy enforcement, and integration with the broader tooling ecosystem.
- `tests/tools/test_transcription_tools.py` — Test suite for the transcription tools module, validating speech-to-text and text-to-speech pipelines, audio format handling, caching mechanisms, and error recovery under various provider configurations.
- `tests/tools/test_tts_gemini.py` — Unit tests for the Google Gemini Text-to-Speech provider integration, verifying audio generation, parameter handling, and error recovery within the Hermes tooling ecosystem.
- `tests/tools/test_tts_kittentts.py` — Unit tests for the local KittenTTS provider in tools/tts_tool.py, focusing on dependency mocking, module-level model caching, and audio file generation stubs.
- `tests/tools/test_video_generation_dynamic_schema.py` — Unit tests validating the dynamic schema generation for the video generation tool, ensuring provider capabilities, parameter constraints, and registry overrides are accurately reflected in the tool's JSON schema.
- `tests/tools/test_video_generation_tool_surface_matrix.py` — Integration tests verifying the routing matrix for video generation across providers, models, and input modalities by stubbing external clients.
- `tests/tools/test_voice_mode.py` — Unit tests for the tools.voice_mode module, designed to run entirely with mocked dependencies to avoid requiring real audio hardware or network connectivity during CI.
- `tests/tools/test_web_tools_config.py` — Unit tests validating the configuration, initialization, and environment variable handling for web backend clients used by Hermes' web tooling ecosystem.
- `tests/tools/test_zombie_process_cleanup.py` — Tests for zombie process cleanup (issue #7131), verifying that subprocesses spawned by tools are properly reaped when agent sessions end or gateway stops.
- `tests/tui_gateway/test_entry_sys_path.py` — Tests for `tui_gateway/entry.py` sys.path hardening (issue #15989), ensuring local CWD directories cannot shadow installed Hermes modules.
- `tests/tui_gateway/test_goal_command.py` — Tests for `/goal` handling in the TUI gateway, verifying routing through `command.dispatch` and correct state persistence via `GoalManager`.
- `tests/tui_gateway/test_make_agent_provider.py` — Regression tests for #11884, ensuring `_make_agent` correctly resolves and forwards runtime provider credentials and API modes to `AIAgent`.
- `tests/tui_gateway/test_protocol.py` — Tests for TUI gateway JSON-RPC protocol plumbing, covering envelope formatting, error handling, session management, and command dispatch routing.
- `tests/tui_gateway/test_render.py` — Tests for `tui_gateway.render` fallback behavior, ensuring graceful degradation when `agent.rich_output` is missing or fails.
- `tests/tui_gateway/test_review_summary_callback.py` — Tests for TUI gateway background-review summary delivery, verifying callback wiring and event emission for self-improvement notifications.
- `tests/website/test_generate_skill_docs.py` — Tests for `website/scripts/generate-skill-docs.py`, ensuring ASCII diagrams in SKILL.md files are wrapped in lint-ignore markers.

## tools

- `tools/__init__.py` — Tools package namespace with minimal import side effects, preventing eager loading of the full tool stack during config initialization.
- `tools/ansi_strip.py` — Strips ANSI escape sequences from subprocess output to prevent terminal control codes from polluting the model's context window.
- `tools/approval.py` — Manages detection and user prompting for dangerous commands, orchestrating approval workflows and tracking per-session or per-command authorization states.
- `tools/browser_providers/browser_use.py` — Browser Use cloud browser provider supporting direct API keys or managed Nous gateway routing. Implements session creation with idempotency keys for managed mode, configurable timeouts/proxies, and graceful cleanup via PATCH requests.
- `tools/browser_providers/browserbase.py` — Browserbase cloud browser provider using direct credentials. Supports optional paid features (proxies, stealth, keep-alive) with automatic 402 fallback retry logic. Manages session lifecycle via POST/DELETE to Browserbase API.
- `tools/browser_providers/firecrawl.py` — Firecrawl cloud browser provider with direct API key authentication. Creates ephemeral browser sessions with configurable TTL, returning CDP URLs for agent interaction. Simple lifecycle with POST creation and DELETE cleanup.
- `tools/browser_supervisor.py` — Persistent CDP WebSocket supervisor managing browser dialogs, frame trees, and console events per task. Injects a dialog bridge script to intercept alert/confirm/prompt calls, supports configurable auto-dismiss/accept policies, and provides thread-safe snapshots for tool handlers.
- `tools/budget_config.py` — Defines immutable budget constants for tool result persistence, including per-result thresholds, per-turn aggregate budgets, and preview sizes. Resolves thresholds via priority chain: pinned values > config overrides > registry defaults.
- `tools/checkpoint_manager.py` — Transparent filesystem snapshot manager leveraging a shared git object store to deduplicate checkpoints across projects. Provides automatic pre-operation snapshots, rollback capabilities, and automated pruning based on retention policies and size caps.
- `tools/clarify_gateway.py` — Gateway-side blocking queue primitive that bridges synchronous agent threads with asynchronous platform adapters for interactive user clarification. Manages pending requests, timeouts, and session-scoped cleanup to prevent thread hangs.
- `tools/clarify_tool.py` — Agent-facing tool schema and dispatcher for interactive clarifying questions. Validates inputs, enforces choice limits, and delegates to a platform-provided callback to handle UI rendering and user input collection.
- `tools/code_execution_tool.py` — Defines a Hermes agent tool for dynamically executing code snippets. Likely manages sandboxed environments, input validation, execution timeouts, and safe output capture to prevent resource exhaustion or security breaches during agent-driven coding tasks.
- `tools/computer_use/tool.py` — Universal macOS desktop control tool that wraps cua-driver's computer-use primitive. Provides standard OpenAI function-calling schema so any model can drive it, with approval gating for destructive actions, hard-blocked key combos, and multimodal capture responses.
- `tools/credential_files.py` — File passthrough registry for remote terminal backends (Docker, Modal, SSH). Manages session-scoped credential file mounts, skill directory sanitization, and cache directory mirroring with path traversal guards and symlink stripping.
- `tools/cronjob_tools.py` — Unified cron job management tool with prompt injection scanning, script path validation, model override pinning, and delivery normalization. Wraps `cron.jobs` functions in a single compressed schema to reduce context bloat.
- `tools/debug_helpers.py` — Shared `DebugSession` class for per-tool debug logging. Activated by tool-specific environment variables, records calls to in-memory lists, and flushes to JSON files in `HERMES_HOME/logs/`.
- `tools/delegate_tool.py` — Implements the subagent delegation architecture, spawning isolated child AIAgent instances with restricted toolsets, dedicated terminal sessions, and configurable approval callbacks. Manages runtime state for pausing, interrupting, and tracking active subagents, while extracting conversation tails for TUI overlay reporting.
- `tools/discord_tool.py` — A tool module for Discord server introspection and management, likely exposing agent-facing capabilities to query or manipulate Discord servers, channels, or roles programmatically.
- `tools/environments/file_sync.py` — Manages transactional file synchronization and modification time tracking for remote execution environments, ensuring consistency between local and remote workspaces.
- `tools/feishu_doc_tool.py` — Provides a tool interface for reading Feishu document content, likely integrating with the Feishu API to fetch and parse document data for agent consumption.
- `tools/feishu_drive_tool.py` — Provides Feishu Drive tools for managing document comments, including listing, replying, and adding comments. Shares the thread-local client injection pattern and lazy-import strategy with other Feishu tools.
- `tools/file_tools.py` — Module providing file manipulation utilities for LLM agents, expected to implement safety gates, pagination, and state coordination for concurrent reads and writes.
- `tools/image_generation_tool.py` — A Python module that serves as the tool handler or registry for AI-driven image generation within the Hermes ecosystem.
- `tools/mcp_oauth_manager.py` — Central manager for per-server MCP OAuth lifecycle, handling token persistence, refresh coordination, and concurrent access isolation. (Note: source content was truncated in the batch input.)
- `tools/mixture_of_agents_tool.py` — Python script implementing a Mixture-of-Agents tool for the Hermes framework. Likely orchestrates multiple sub-agents or models to collaboratively solve tasks, though the provided snippet is truncated.
- `tools/registry.py` — Central registry module for managing and discovering Hermes agent tools, handling dynamic schema overrides, tool definitions, and lifecycle coordination with the agent loop and plugin system.
- `tools/send_message_tool.py` — Defines a tool for cross-channel messaging, designed to route outbound communications through Hermes' platform adapter infrastructure.
- `tools/skill_manager_tool.py` — Provides a programmatic or CLI interface for managing agent skills, likely handling installation, updates, or configuration within the Hermes ecosystem.
- `tools/skills_tool.py` — Implements progressive-disclosure skill tools (skills_list, skill_view) that scan local and external skill directories, parse YAML frontmatter, check platform compatibility, validate prerequisites, and serve content with security guards against path traversal and prompt injection.
- `tools/slash_confirm.py` — Gateway-side confirmation primitive for expensive slash commands. Supports button UI and text-fallback delivery paths, with module-level state storage, timeout-based staleness checks, and async/sync resolution helpers.
- `tools/terminal_tool.py` — A multi-backend terminal execution tool supporting local, Docker, Modal, and Vercel Sandbox environments. It provides safe environment variable parsing, Vercel credential validation, disk usage monitoring, and thread-local sudo password caching to isolate concurrent ACP sessions.
- `tools/tirith_security.py` — A Python wrapper module that integrates Tirith security scanning into the pre-execution pipeline, likely validating code or commands before they are run by the agent.
- `tools/tts_tool.py` — A Python module implementing text-to-speech (TTS) functionality for the Hermes agent ecosystem. It likely handles audio synthesis and bridges with platform adapters for voice delivery.
- `tools/video_generation_tool.py` — A Python module intended to handle video generation tasks, likely bridging agent requests with external media synthesis providers. The visible snippet shows a standard executable header and docstring, indicating it serves as a tool handler within the Hermes ecosystem.
- `tools/web_tools.py` — A standalone Python module intended to provide web-related utility functions or tools, as indicated by its initial docstring.

## tui_gateway

- `tui_gateway/transport.py` — Abstracts JSON-RPC transport for the TUI gateway, decoupling frame serialization from direct stdout writes to support flexible I/O backends.

## ui-tui

- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/components/App.tsx` — Root React component for the Hermes terminal UI, responsible for initializing global state hooks like interaction timestamp tracking and orchestrating the main component tree for Ink-based rendering.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/components/Box.tsx` — A foundational layout component for the Hermes Ink terminal UI, built with React. It imports standard React types and leverages the React Compiler runtime for optimized re-rendering in a CLI environment.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/components/TerminalFocusContext.tsx` — React context provider that exposes terminal window focus state via useSyncExternalStore, isolating focus-driven re-renders from the main App component tree.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/components/TerminalSizeContext.tsx` — Minimal React context for terminal dimensions (columns/rows), initialized to null and populated by a provider elsewhere in the tree.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/components/Text.test.ts` — Unit tests for ANSI dim detection and Apple Terminal fallback color logic, verifying terminal environment heuristics and explicit env overrides.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/components/Text.tsx` — Styled text component supporting color, weight, decoration, and wrap modes, with terminal-aware dim handling that falls back to hex color on incompatible terminals.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/constants.ts` — Shared frame timing constants for render throttling, keeping blurred animations at full speed when keepAlive subscribers exist.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/cursor.ts` — Type definition for cursor state tracking position (x, y) and visibility in the terminal screen buffer.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/devtools.ts` — Placeholder module for optional React DevTools integration, exporting nothing to avoid hard dependencies.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/dom.ts` — Virtual DOM implementation for Ink, managing element creation, tree mutations, style diffing, text measurement caching, and dirty propagation for Yoga layout.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/click-event.ts` — Mouse click event class with screen coordinates, local-relative offsets, and blank-cell detection for ignoring clicks on empty terminal space.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/cmd-shortcuts.test.ts` — Tests for enhanced keyboard modifier parsing, verifying CSI-u and modifyOtherKeys sequences correctly expose Cmd/Super and Shift/Ctrl modifiers.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/dispatcher.ts` — Event dispatcher implementing React-style two-phase (capture/bubble) propagation with priority-based scheduling for discrete and continuous events.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/emitter.ts` — EventEmitter wrapper that respects React-style `stopImmediatePropagation()` on the first argument when it's an Event instance.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/event-handlers.ts` — Type definitions and lookup tables mapping terminal event types to React-style handler prop names for capture and bubble phases.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/event.ts` — Minimal base Event class providing `stopImmediatePropagation()` and `didStopImmediatePropagation()` for propagation control.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/focus-event.ts` — Focus event class for component focus changes, carrying the related target and bubbling to match browser focusin/focusout semantics.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/input-event.ts` — Input event parser handling CSI-u, modifyOtherKeys, and application keypad sequences, with defensive guards against escape sequence fragmentation.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/keyboard-event.ts` — Keyboard event class mirroring browser KeyboardEvent semantics, with literal character keys for printable keys and named keys for specials.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/mouse-event.ts` — Mouse event class with screen coordinates, local-relative offsets, blank-cell detection, and button identifier.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/paste-event.ts` — Paste event class carrying the pasted text payload, bubbling and cancelable like browser paste events.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/resize-event.ts` — Resize event class carrying new terminal dimensions (columns/rows), bubbling and cancelable.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/terminal-event.ts` — Base terminal event class implementing DOM-style propagation with target, currentTarget, eventPhase, stopPropagation, and preventDefault.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/events/terminal-focus-event.ts` — Terminal window focus event for DECSET 1004 focus reporting, distinguishing terminal-level focus from component-level focus.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/focus.ts` — DOM-like focus manager tracking active element, focus stack for Tab navigation, and tree-aware focus restoration when nodes are removed.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/frame.ts` — Frame type definitions and screen-clearing logic for the render pipeline, with detailed phase timing instrumentation.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/get-max-width.ts` — Utility to extract a Yoga node's content width by subtracting padding and border, with a warning about cross-axis overflow in column layouts.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/global.d.ts` — Empty TypeScript module declaration file that ensures the ink package is treated as a module by the TypeScript compiler, enabling top-level imports without namespace pollution.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hit-test.ts` — Implements hit-testing and event dispatch for the terminal DOM. Finds the deepest DOM element at a given (col, row) coordinate using cached rects, then bubbles ClickEvent, MouseEvent, and hover events up the parent chain with propagation control.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-animation-frame.ts` — React hook for synchronized animations that pause when offscreen. Subscribes to a shared ClockContext and useTerminalViewport visibility, returning a ref and elapsed time. Animations auto-slow when the terminal is blurred.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-app.ts` — Thin React hook that exposes the AppContext, providing access to a method for manually exiting (unmounting) the Ink application.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-declared-cursor.ts` — React hook that declares where the terminal cursor should be parked after each frame. Uses CursorDeclarationContext and nodeCache rects to position the cursor relative to a Box, with careful handling of sibling handoff and memoization hazards.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-external-process.ts` — React hook and utility for running external processes while temporarily suspending the Ink TUI. Enters alternate screen before execution and exits afterward, preserving the TUI state.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-input.ts` — React hook for handling user keyboard input. Registers a stable listener on the stdin emitter, manages raw mode toggling via useLayoutEffect, and respects isActive gating and Ctrl+C exit behavior.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-interval.ts` — React hooks backed by the shared ClockContext for time-based updates. `useAnimationTimer` returns clock time at a given interval; `useInterval` fires a callback at intervals. Both piggyback on the single shared clock.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-search-highlight.ts` — React hook for screen-space search highlighting. Sets a query that inverts all visible occurrences on the next frame, and supports position-based current-match highlighting with scroll offset tracking.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-selection.ts` — React hooks for text selection operations in fullscreen mode. `useSelection` exposes copy, clear, shift, and capture methods; `useHasSelection` provides reactive selection-exists state via useSyncExternalStore.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-stdin.ts` — Thin React hook that exposes the StdinContext, providing access to the stdin stream for components that need direct terminal input handling.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-tab-status.ts` — React hook for declaratively setting the terminal tab-status indicator via OSC 21337. Emits colored dots and status text for idle/busy/waiting states, with automatic cleanup on null transitions.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-terminal-focus.ts` — React hook that reports whether the terminal currently has focus, using DECSET 1004 focus reporting escape sequences handled automatically by Ink.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-terminal-title.ts` — React hook for declaratively setting the terminal tab/window title. Strips ANSI escapes automatically, uses OSC 0 on Unix and process.title on Windows.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hooks/use-terminal-viewport.ts` — React hook for detecting if a component is within the terminal viewport. Returns a ref and visibility entry, updated during layout phase without triggering re-renders to avoid infinite loops.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/hyperlinkHover.ts` — Post-layout utility that highlights every screen cell whose OSC 8 hyperlink matches a hovered URL by inverting its style. Used as a cursor-hover affordance for clickable links in terminal applications.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/instances.ts` — Module-level Map that stores all Ink instances keyed by their stdout WriteStream, ensuring consecutive render() calls reuse the same instance and allowing unmount cleanup from a separate module.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/layout/engine.ts` — Factory function that creates layout nodes by delegating to the Yoga layout engine. Provides a single entry point for layout node creation.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/layout/geometry.ts` — Geometry types and utility functions for the layout system. Defines Point, Size, Rectangle, and Edges types with helpers for edge manipulation, rectangle operations, and clamping.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/layout/node.ts` — Adapter interface for the layout engine. Defines const enums for flexbox properties (direction, align, justify, wrap, overflow, position) and the LayoutNode interface with tree, style, and lifecycle methods.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/layout/yoga.ts` — Yoga layout engine adapter implementing the LayoutNode interface. Wraps Yoga.Node instances, mapping Ink's layout enums to Yoga's internal enums and delegating all layout operations.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/line-width-cache.ts` — LRU cache for string width measurements. During streaming, completed lines are immutable, so caching per-line width avoids re-measuring hundreds of unchanged lines on every token (~50x reduction).
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/log-update.test.ts` — Contract tests for LogUpdate.render() verifying diff-to-ANSI rendering invariants. Tests cover changed-cell emission, width-change clearTerminal patches, identical-frame no-op behavior, and scrollback-only change handling.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/log-update.ts` — Core diff-to-ANSI renderer that computes minimal terminal updates between frames. Handles scroll optimization via DECSTBM, scrollback detection, wide-character compensation, and full-reset fallbacks for unreachable changes.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/lru.ts` — Shared LRU eviction utility for Ink's hot-path caches. Provides bulk eviction by keeping a ratio of entries, deleting from the beginning of the Map (oldest).
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/measure-element.ts` — Measures the computed width and height of a DOM element by reading its Yoga layout node's dimensions, defaulting to zero if the Yoga node is absent.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/measure-text.ts` — Single-pass text measurement utility that computes both width and wrapped height for a string given a max width, using `indexOf` to avoid array allocations from `split('\n')`.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/node-cache.ts` — WeakMap-based cache for layout bounds and pending clear regions, plus a module-level flag tracking absolute-positioned node removals to disable blit optimization when cross-subtree paint overlap is possible.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/optimizer.ts` — Single-pass diff optimizer that reduces terminal patch count by removing no-ops, merging consecutive cursor moves, concatenating style patches, deduping hyperlinks, and canceling cursor hide/show pairs.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/output.ts` — Core terminal rendering engine that collects write/blit/clear/clip/shift/noSelect operations, applies them to a Screen buffer with clipping and absolute-node ghost prevention, and caches grapheme-clustered characters across frames.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/parse-keypress.test.ts` — Unit tests for bracketed paste recovery, mouse wheel modifier decoding, and fragmented SGR mouse event re-synthesis in the keypress parser.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/parse-keypress.ts` — Terminal input parser that tokenizes escape sequences into keypresses, mouse events, and terminal responses, supporting Kitty keyboard protocol, xterm modifyOtherKeys, SGR/X10 mouse, bracketed paste, and fragmented SGR mouse recovery.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/reconciler.ts` — React reconciler configuration for Ink that bridges React's virtual DOM to terminal DOM elements, handling node creation, prop diffing, Yoga layout cleanup, focus management, and event dispatching.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/render-border.ts` — Renders box borders with configurable styles, per-edge colors, dimming, and embedded text positioning (top/bottom borders with start/end/center alignment).
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/render-node-to-output.ts` — Converts a virtual DOM node tree into terminal-compatible output strings, applying indentation and text styling for the Hermes TUI rendering pipeline.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/screen.ts` — A TypeScript module establishing screen geometry types and ANSI code utilities for a custom Ink-based terminal UI renderer.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/styles.ts` — Centralizes layout and styling type imports for the Hermes TUI, bridging React component props with Ink's underlying Yoga layout engine to ensure consistent terminal rendering.
- `ui-tui/packages/hermes-ink/src/ink/termio/types.ts` — Defines semantic TypeScript interfaces for an ANSI escape sequence parser in the Hermes Ink TUI, abstracting raw terminal control codes into structured data for rendering and manipulation.
- `ui-tui/src/__tests__/constants.test.ts` — A Vitest test suite designed to validate UI constant definitions, specifically importing face/emoji mappings and keyboard hotkey configurations for verification.
- `ui-tui/src/__tests__/createGatewayEventHandler.test.ts` — Unit tests for the TUI's gateway event handler, focusing on how todo lists are archived into the transcript and how turn state persists across message boundaries.
- `ui-tui/src/__tests__/platform.test.ts` — Vitest test suite designed to mock `process.platform` for cross-platform TUI component testing, ensuring consistent behavior across different operating systems.
- `ui-tui/src/__tests__/useConfigSync.test.ts` — Vitest unit tests for a configuration synchronization hook in the TUI, focusing on UI state management and reset behaviors.
- `ui-tui/src/app/slash/commands/ops.ts` — Declares TypeScript type imports for operational slash command responses in the Hermes TUI, covering browser management, command catalogs, delegation pausing, process termination, and environment reloading.
- `ui-tui/src/app/useMainApp.ts` — Custom React hook orchestrating the main TUI application lifecycle, integrating Ink terminal utilities with Nanostores state management for selection handling, stdout access, and dynamic terminal title updates.
- `ui-tui/src/components/appOverlays.tsx` — React component for rendering application overlays in the Hermes TUI, leveraging Ink for terminal layout and Nanostores for reactive state, while integrating with the gateway context.
- `ui-tui/src/components/textInput.tsx` — A React-based terminal UI text input component built on a custom Ink framework fork, managing keyboard events, focus state, and rendering for interactive CLI interfaces.
- `ui-tui/src/hooks/useCompletion.ts` — A custom React hook for managing completion state and logic in a terminal UI, importing React hooks and utilities for slash command detection.
- `ui-tui/src/hooks/useGitBranch.ts` — Polls and caches the current Git branch for a given working directory, deduplicating concurrent requests and refreshing every 15 seconds.
- `ui-tui/src/hooks/useInputHistory.ts` — Provides React state and refs for managing command input history, delegating persistence to a shared history module.
- `ui-tui/src/hooks/useQueue.ts` — Manages a mutable string queue with React state synchronization, providing utilities for in-place removal and editing.
- `ui-tui/src/lib/openExternalUrl.ts` — A TypeScript utility module that uses Node.js APIs to spawn OS-specific processes for opening external URLs in the user's default browser or application.

## web

- `web/src/components/SidebarFooter.tsx` — A React component that renders the sidebar footer, leveraging a custom status hook, typography primitives, and a CSS class merger utility.
- `web/src/components/SidebarStatusStrip.tsx` — React component intended to display gateway or agent status information within the sidebar, though the provided source is truncated.
- `web/src/components/SlashPopover.tsx` — Implements an autocomplete popover for slash commands in the chat composer, mirroring TUI completion behavior with keyboard navigation.
- `web/src/i18n/en.ts` — Defines the English language translation dictionary for the web frontend's internationalization system, exporting common UI strings such as 'Save' and 'Saving...' for consistent text rendering across the application.
- `web/src/i18n/hu.ts` — Hungarian localization file defining UI string translations for the web frontend, implementing the shared Translations type contract.
- `web/src/i18n/pt.ts` — Portuguese localization dictionary for the web frontend's i18n system, exporting a typed object containing UI strings for common actions like saving and canceling.
- `web/src/i18n/uk.ts` — Ukrainian localization dictionary for the web frontend, providing translated UI strings mapped to the project's shared translation schema.
- `web/src/pages/AnalyticsPage.tsx` — A React page component intended for an analytics dashboard, importing hooks and UI icons for sorting and charting. The provided source is truncated after the import block.
- `web/src/pages/EnvPage.tsx` — React page component for managing environment variables or configuration settings in the Hermes web UI, leveraging standard hooks and iconography for interactive controls.
- `web/src/pages/SessionsPage.tsx` — React component page for managing user sessions, initializing core hooks for state, effects, callbacks, and programmatic navigation within the web frontend.

## website

- `website/scripts/generate-llms-txt.py` — A Python utility that generates `llms.txt` and `llms-full.txt` files for the Hermes documentation website. It curates and indexes documentation content into machine-readable formats optimized for LLM consumption, outputting the results to the `website/static/` directory.
